# The Simpsons (MAME set 'simpsons') - Z80 sound program symbols (072-e03.6g)
#
# Addresses and names only: no game data lives in this file. Format: ADDRESS KIND NAME [...] (see
# tools/core/z80/analysis.py and tools/core/sound_coverage.py). Addresses are the Z80's, 0000-7FFF.
#   func     a function entry (one nothing calls is decompiled too: code the entry points never reach)
#   loop     the main loop's top, a function entry: where the program waits for the board (runtime/src/sound_cpu.c
#            runs the main loop from there while the board's time is ahead)
#   file     PATH: the functions from ADDRESS up to the next file line go to src/sound/PATH.c (the comment of a
#            file's first line is its description)
#   comment FUNCTION TEXT: what the C says above the function; register RN NAME: a register the whole program keeps
#   (game_profile's SOUND_CONTEXT), a global variable in C; unread FUNCTION RN: the function reads RN before setting
#   it only on a path the program never takes (it is no parameter)
#
# The driver (read from the listing, build/sound/listing.s): the 053248 writes a command and its argument to the
# K053260's ports (1FC6-1FC7, which the Z80 reads at FC00-FC01) and raises the Z80's IRQ by reading 1FC4; the Z80
# takes it only in the main loop's four NOPs with interrupts on (sound_command, 0038): F0-FF the self test's answers
# (FC02-FC03) and the reset, 3F and 7F with the argument, the rest a sound and the argument's count of the ones after
# it (start_sound): 00 stops everything, 01-38 an FM effect, 40-7E a PCM effect, 80-AF a song, C0-EF a song after a
# fade. The main loop waits for the YM2151's timer B (some 2 ms), then runs 12 channel records of 50h bytes from F003:
# 8 for the YM2151's channels (chip_channel, +40, 0-7) and 4 for the K053260's voices (8-B), each through the handler
# it keeps at +43 (run_channel, JP (HL) at 0C21) after the music's tick (music_tick); +41 is the sound a record plays
# (bit 7: music), +42 its priority (struct channel below). A PCM voice is keyed on after a HALT that waits for the
# K053260's NMI (pcm_key_on).

# ======== the driver ========
0000  file  main  # the reset and its self test, the commands from the 053248, the main loop
0000  func  sound_reset
comment sound_reset The reset: interrupt mode 1, the stack at the top of the RAM, no answer to the 053248 yet; the RAM test (55, then AA, written and read back in every byte: bit 0 of self_test_failures when one fails), then check_rom.
0038  func  sound_command
comment sound_command The interrupt the 053248 raises: its command and argument from the K053260's ports (unless held_command holds one, which nothing sets). F0 answers 30, F1 to F7 hand back self_test_failures and the bytes of the three sums, FE adds the two sample ROMs up again, FF resets; while the RAM test has failed nothing else runs (self_test_failed); F8 counts in command_f8_count, FD fades the music out. 3F starts the PCM effect of effect_handlers_2 the argument picks on a K053260 record, 7F hands the argument to the record playing effect 7F; any other command goes to start_sound, and so do as many commands after it as the argument says.
0066  func  nmi_return
comment nmi_return The NMI (the K053260's, armed by a write to the NMI's port): returns at once; it only ends pcm_key_on's wait.
# Odd: the commands after the first one (008D-0096 "inc b; push af; push bc; call $0099; pop bc; pop af; inc a; djnz")
# skip sound_command's own tests, so a 3E with an argument of 1 would hand 3F to start_sound, which plays the unused
# group's effect 3F (01E5) rather than a PCM effect of the third group; no command the game sends does that
0099  func  start_sound
comment start_sound A command 00 to EF. 00: stop_all_channels. 01-38: an FM effect, on the YM2151 record already playing it, else on the one of the lowest priority if the effect's (effect_priorities) is not below it: its sound and priority, its handler from effect_handlers, the loudest volume, no LFO, keyed off, released at the fastest, no noise. 3C-3F: the unused group on FM_7 (no such command is sent). 40-7E: a PCM effect the same way on the K053260's records (effect_priorities_40, effect_handlers_40). 80-AF: start_song. C0-EF: the song 40 below, at once when no record plays music, else once the music has faded out (fade, fade_next_song).
00B2  func  stop_all_channels
comment stop_all_channels Command 00: stops every record but those of priority C0 or more (stop_channel_unless_kept), then the music (stop_music).
# Odd: stop_music leaves 0 in A (015A "xor a"), and the main loop compares the fade's attenuation in A after it
# (0BC7-0BCE "cp $7F; call z,$0106; cp $30"): at the fade's end the test for 30 sees 0, so stopping the music never
# also starts fade_next_song (the two cannot meet anyway: 30 comes before 7F)
0106  func  stop_music
comment stop_music The music off on all 12 records (stop_music_channel) and the fade cleared: at the fade's end, and after command 00.
016B  func  stop_channel_unless_kept
comment stop_channel_unless_kept stop_channel, unless the record's priority is C0 or more (PCM effects 40 to 42).
034E  func  start_song
comment start_song A song, commands 80-AF: its bank (song_banks_by_sound) into the bank register, each record's track from the song's header (start_music_channel, FM_0 to PCM_3), the fade cleared.
03CF  func  start_music_channel
comment start_music_channel The record's track from the song header's next word (start_music_track); hands back the place of the word after it.
095C  func  check_rom
comment check_rom The ROM test: adds up the program's 32 KB and the banks 2 to 7 into program_rom_sum; a sum other than 072E sets bit 1 of self_test_failures; then sound_main. The game's sound board puts its own in its place (the game has no ROM to add up: it takes the time good chips take).
09FB  func  sound_main
comment sound_main Clears the RAM, sets the K053260 up (its voices' registers cleared, every key off), gives each record its chip_channel and the K053260's records their voice's registers and bits; then, unless the RAM test failed, stops every channel and starts the YM2151's timer B: the main loop follows.
0B28  func  self_test_failed
comment self_test_failed The RAM test failed: forever a delay and a window with interrupts on, where the 053248's commands come in (and end here again).
0B3D  loop  sound_main_loop
comment sound_main_loop A pass of the main loop: waits for the YM2151's timer B and starts it again; runs the 8 YM2151 records (run_channel) and the 4 K053260 ones (run_pcm_channel); the fade between songs (every fade_period passes fade_attenuation one more: at 30 fade_next_song starts, at 7F the music stops; a fade other than 1 only counts fade_hold down); then a window of four instructions with interrupts on, where the 053248's command comes in, sound_command again if command_held says so (nothing sets it), and pass_count.
0BF3  func  run_pcm_channel
comment run_pcm_channel A K053260 record's pass: on one pass in 16 (its own, by its chip_channel), while its voice is not keyed, the decay one decay_step quieter (up to 7F) and update_volume; then run_channel.
0C18  func  run_channel
comment run_channel A record's pass: its music (music_tick), then its handler.
0C22  file  chips  # the records' handlers and the chips: the YM2151's registers, patches, volumes, pitches, keys, LFO and pan; the K053260's voices
0C22  func  stop_channel
comment stop_channel Stops what the record plays: key off, the fastest release, its sound and priority 0, its handler channel_idle.
0C33  func  set_channel_handler
comment set_channel_handler The routine that runs the record from its next pass on.
0C39  func  channel_idle
comment channel_idle A free record's handler: nothing.
0C3A  func  write_fm_channel_register
comment write_fm_channel_register Writes a register of the record's YM2151 channel: the register given plus its chip_channel.
0C3F  func  write_fm_register
comment write_fm_register Writes a register of the YM2151, each byte once the chip's busy flag is clear.
0C56  func  restart_frame_timer
comment restart_frame_timer Starts the YM2151's timer B again (register 12 at F9: some 2 ms) and clears its flag: the main loop's pace.
0C68  func  load_patch
comment load_patch Loads a patch into the record's channel: on the YM2151 (keyed off and released at the fastest first) its 25 bytes into the operators' registers, the connection, the total levels and the release rates kept in the record, then update_volume; on the K053260, pcm_load_patch.
# each chip routine's K053260 half, past its "ld a,(ix+$40); sub $08; jp nc" (or "cp $08"): a function of its own, so
# that what it reads in the record reads as a K053260 voice's (scoped channel pcm)
0D2E  func  pcm_load_patch
comment pcm_load_patch A K053260 voice's patch: the voice keyed off, its sample's registers (samples) into the voice's, the voice's KADPCM bit as the sample's kadpcm says, the patch's level and decay_step into the record, then update_volume.
0D72  func  set_volume
comment set_volume The record's volume (an attenuation: 0 the loudest, 7F silent), then update_volume.
0D75  func  update_volume
comment update_volume The record's volume to its chip: on the YM2151 the four operators' total levels, the volume added to the carriers' (carrier_masks, by the algorithm in connection); on the K053260, pcm_update_volume.
0DC0  func  pcm_update_volume
comment pcm_update_volume A K053260 voice's volume register: 7F less the volume, the patch's level and the decay (each sum at most 7F), and 0 below 4.
0DA7  func  write_operator_level
comment write_operator_level One operator's total level: the volume added (at most 7F) when the carriers' mask says it is a carrier; hands the mask back shifted for the next operator.
0DE8  func  set_pitch
comment set_pitch The record's pitch to its chip: on the YM2151 the key fraction and the key code (note_codes; its high byte's octaves wrap past 8); on the K053260, pcm_set_pitch.
0E70  func  pcm_set_pitch
comment pcm_set_pitch A K053260 voice's pitch registers.
0E7B  func  key_on
comment key_on Keys the record's channel on: on the YM2151 its four operators; on the K053260, pcm_key_on.
0E8B  func  pcm_key_on
comment pcm_key_on Keys a K053260 voice on, unless pcm_keyed says it is: its key bit off, then the NMI armed and a wait for it (the chip takes the new sample), the key bit on, pcm_keyed set, the decay back to 0 and update_volume.
0EBE  func  key_off
comment key_off Keys the record's channel off: on the YM2151 its four operators; on the K053260, pcm_key_off.
0ECC  func  pcm_key_off
comment pcm_key_off Clears a K053260 voice's bit of pcm_keyed, if set: its sample plays on to its end.
0EDD  func  fast_release
comment fast_release On the YM2151 the four operators' release at its fastest; on the K053260, pcm_fast_release.
0F01  func  pcm_fast_release
comment pcm_fast_release A K053260 voice's decay at 7F: silent at the next volume.
0F06  func  restore_release
comment restore_release Unused: puts a YM2151 channel's release rates back as its patch had them (after fast_release).
0F2C  func  set_lfo_pm_depth
comment set_lfo_pm_depth The YM2151's LFO phase modulation depth (register 19 with bit 7).
0F34  func  set_lfo_am_depth
comment set_lfo_am_depth The YM2151's LFO amplitude modulation depth (register 19 without bit 7).
0F3C  func  set_channel_pm_sensitivity
comment set_channel_pm_sensitivity A YM2151 channel's phase modulation sensitivity (register 38's bits 4-6), kept in lfo_sensitivity; nothing on the K053260.
0F58  func  set_channel_am_sensitivity
comment set_channel_am_sensitivity A YM2151 channel's amplitude modulation sensitivity (register 38's bits 0-1), kept in lfo_sensitivity; nothing on the K053260.
0F70  func  set_lfo_frequency
comment set_lfo_frequency The YM2151's LFO frequency (register 18).
0F76  func  set_lfo_waveform
comment set_lfo_waveform The YM2151's LFO waveform (register 1B).
0F7C  func  set_pan
comment set_pan The record's pan: a YM2151 channel's outputs (1 left, 2 right, 3 both: register 20's bits 6-7, kept in connection), or a K053260 voice's pan (1 left to 7 right) in its pan register, kept in pcm_pans_1 or pcm_pans_2.
0FE5  func  noise_off
comment noise_off On FM_7, the YM2151's noise off (register 0F).
0FF2  func  noise_on
comment noise_on Unused: on FM_7, the YM2151's noise on at the frequency given (register 0F).
1000  func  pcm_voice_playing
comment pcm_voice_playing Whether the record's K053260 voice still plays (its bit of the chip's status); a YM2151 record says it does.
100D  func  next_pointer_byte
comment next_pointer_byte Unused: the byte at byte_pointer, which moves on by one within its page (sound_main points it at the program's bytes).

# the self test's results, which the commands F1-F7 hand to the 053248: failures (bit 0 the RAM, 0958; bit 1 the program
# ROM, 09F8; bits 2 and 3 the sample ROMs, 0783 and 0831), the program ROM's sum (09E6-09ED, 072E on good chips) and the
# two sample ROMs' sums (0771-0778 and 081F-0826)
register a_alt self_test_failures
register bc_alt program_rom_sum
register de_alt sample_rom_1_sum
register hl_alt sample_rom_2_sum
# the lowest priority channel's record goes to HL from the first channel on (019C, 02B0, 0251, 0310: any priority is at
# most FF), so HL is never read as it came in (01A6, 02BA, 025B, 031A)
unread sound_command hl
unread start_sound hl

# ======== the sound effects ========
# the handler a sound effect's channel runs, by the effect's number, 01-38 (01BB "SLA C; LD B,0; LD HL,$1014; ADD
# HL,BC", into the channel's word at 43; kref --sound-rom-reads: 01C3 reads 1016-1085), and the effect's priority
# (01AC "LD HL,$1085; ADD HL,DE", compared with the channel's at 42: 01B0 reads 1086-10BD); entry 0 is no effect
1014  jumps  effect_handlers  57  first=1
1086  data   effect_priorities  u8[56]
# the same for the effects 40-7F (02BD-02DD, 032F-033D: the table at 3570 and the priorities at 3630, from entry 40
# on: 02D7 reads 35F0-366D, 0337 366E-366F, 02C4 3670-36AE)
3570  jumps  effect_handlers_40  128  first=64
3670  data   effect_priorities_40  u8[64]
# and a third group (0270-027E: the table at 2E01, the priorities at 2E79 from 0261; 0278 reads 2E01-2E78, 0265
# 2E79-2EB4: 60 of each)
2E01  jumps  effect_handlers_2  60
2E79  data   effect_priorities_2  u8[60]
# start_sound takes the commands 3C-3E at 01E5 (sound_command takes 3F first, 0083): a handler at 26FC plus twice the
# command and a priority at 2740 plus the command (01F0, 0205), which are unused_effect_group and
# unused_effect_priorities below; the table from 26FC is the code there (26FC's words 0C22 0CFC 1F28...) up to them, a
# table of no entries here. No command the game sends reaches it (kref --sound-rom-reads: nothing reads them)
26FC  jumps  unused_effect_handlers  0

# ======== the music ========
# a sequence's commands E0-FF (4659 "LD L,C; LD H,0; ADD HL,HL; LD DE,$44A6; ADD HL,DE", then 4665 "JP (HL)"): the table
# the code indexes starts at 44A6, its entries from E0 on (kref --sound-rom-reads: 4661 reads 4666-46A4)
44A6  jumps  music_commands  256  first=224
4665  via    music_commands
# an instrument's 16 routines, by the low 4 bits of a note (45FD "AND $0F; RLCA", 4604 "LD L,(IX+$28)", 460B "LD
# A,(HL)", then 460F "CALL $0C33", which stores it at 43 in the channel): the table of the channel's instrument
# (46BB "LD DE,$4E4E", its field 12 times 32: 46C9 "LD (IX+$28),L"); instrument 0's would be the code before 4E6E, and
# each one's routine 0 is none (0000). kref --sound-rom-reads: 460B reads 4E70-4FCC (instruments 1-11)
4E4E  jumps  instrument_routines  192  first=16  slot=43
# two more instruments' routines before them, at 496C and 498C (16 each, routine 0 none): a channel's pointer at 28 is
# 496C or 498C by a way the walk does not see (4E4E plus 32 times its field 12 never is); kref --sound-rom-reads: 460B
# reads 4970, and the routines it leads to run (4A18...)
496C  jumps  instrument_routines_low  32  slot=43
# a song, by the command 80-AF (034E "CP $B0"): its header's address (0362 "LD DE,$5CB2", "ADD HL,HL": entries 80 on)
# and the bank it is in (0355 "LD DE,$5D92", into FE00); kref --sound-rom-reads: 0366 reads 5DB2-5E11, 0359
# 5E12-5E41 (tools/sound_data_walk.py walks the songs)
5DB2  data   song_headers  u16[48]
5E12  data   song_banks  u8[48]

# ======== the YM2151 ========
# which of the 4 operators an algorithm's carriers are, a bit each (0D80 "AND $07; ...; LD HL,$0DB8; ADD HL,BC",
# shifted out by 0DA7 "SLA L": the operators whose volume the channel's adds to)
0DB8  data   carrier_masks  u8[8]
# the YM2151's key code by note (0E06 "LD HL,$0E10; ADD HL,BC", into register 28): 8 octaves of 12
0E10  data   note_codes  u8[96]
# a YM2151 patch: load_patch 0C76-0D23 reads it a byte at a time ("inc hl; ld c,(hl)") and writes each to its register
# of the record's channel (the "ld b,$NN" before write_fm_channel_register), operators M1, C1, M2, C2; the total
# levels it keeps in the record instead (update_volume writes them with the volume), the release rates as well
struct fm_patch 19 rom
# 0C76 "ld c,(hl); ld (ix+$46),c; ld b,$20": register 20, the outputs, the feedback and the algorithm
field fm_patch 00 connection u8
# 0C80-0CA5 (registers 40; total level into +47; 80, A0, C0; E0 into +4B)
field fm_patch 01 m1_detune_multiple u8
field fm_patch 02 m1_total_level u8
field fm_patch 03 m1_attack u8
field fm_patch 04 m1_decay_1 u8
field fm_patch 05 m1_decay_2 u8
field fm_patch 06 m1_release u8
# 0CAB-0CD0 (50; +48; 90, B0, D0; F0 into +4C)
field fm_patch 07 c1_detune_multiple u8
field fm_patch 08 c1_total_level u8
field fm_patch 09 c1_attack u8
field fm_patch 0A c1_decay_1 u8
field fm_patch 0B c1_decay_2 u8
field fm_patch 0C c1_release u8
# 0CD6-0CFB (48; +49; 88, A8, C8; E8 into +4D)
field fm_patch 0D m2_detune_multiple u8
field fm_patch 0E m2_total_level u8
field fm_patch 0F m2_attack u8
field fm_patch 10 m2_decay_1 u8
field fm_patch 11 m2_decay_2 u8
field fm_patch 12 m2_release u8
# 0D01-0D26 (58; +4A; 98, B8, D8; F8 into +4E)
field fm_patch 13 c2_detune_multiple u8
field fm_patch 14 c2_total_level u8
field fm_patch 15 c2_attack u8
field fm_patch 16 c2_decay_1 u8
field fm_patch 17 c2_decay_2 u8
field fm_patch 18 c2_release u8
# a K053260 patch (pcm_load_patch 0D3B "ld l,(hl)": its sample, of samples; then two bytes into the record: 0D66 "ld
# (ix+$46),a", 0D6C "ld (ix+$47),a", the level and the decay's step)
struct pcm_patch 03 rom
field pcm_patch 00 sample u8
field pcm_patch 01 level u8
field pcm_patch 02 decay_step u8
# a sample of the K053260 (pcm_load_patch 0D3B-0D4E "LD L,(HL); ...; ADD HL,HL x3; LD DE,$28E9; ADD HL,DE; LD BC,7;
# LDIR" into its voice's registers: pitch, length, start), and bit 0 of the 8th (0D56 "bit 0,(hl)"): the voice's KADPCM
# bit of FC2A (MAME's k053260.cpp, register 2A: bits 0-3 the voices' loop, 4-7 their KADPCM); it is 1 for the samples
# assets/sounds/samples.txt calls kadpcm, and no sample loops (no code sets a loop bit)
struct sample 08 rom
field sample 00 registers u8[7]
field sample 07 kadpcm u8
# the music's instruments (46E0-4704, by the command that sets one): an FM channel's patch (46E2 "LD HL,$5E42"), a
# PCM channel's (46FA "LD HL,$63F4") and its transposition (46ED "LD HL,$642C"), into the channel's 19 and 26
5E42  data   music_fm_patches  u16[54]  fm_patch
63F4  data   music_pcm_patches  u16[28]  pcm_patch
642C  data   music_pcm_transpositions  s16[28]
28E9  data   samples  sample[163]
# the pitches by note and octave (42FE "LD HL,$448B" for an FM channel, 4309 "LD HL,$4533" for a PCM one; then the
# note's low nibble twice, its octave's offset and the transposition): kref --sound-rom-reads, 431E reads 44A5-45DB;
# the FM table's first 13 words would be the code before 44A5, the PCM one runs up to the code at 45F5
44A5  data   fm_pitches  u16[71]
4533  data   pcm_pitches  u16[97]
# a note's length by its high nibble (48DC "AND $F0; RRCA x3; ...; LD HL,$48FA; ADD HL,DE"): notes are 00-DF, 14 of
# them (the code at 4916 follows)
48FA  data   note_lengths  u16[14]
# by an effect's level 1-7 (4198-41A5 "LD DE,$4166; ADD HL,DE" by twice the level: 41A5 reads 4168-4174; level 0
# ends the effect first, 419A), two bytes: a pan (41A7 "CALL $0F7C", set_pan) and a volume (41AB "JP $0D72",
# set_volume)
4168  data   effect_levels  u16[7]
struct effect_level 02 rom
field effect_level 00 pan u8
field effect_level 01 volume u8
# the pitches FM effect 08 steps through, one every 24 passes (fm_effect_08_retrigger 1323-1330, from the record's word
# at 00; kref --sound-rom-reads: 1329 reads 12E1-12EF); the PCM effects 40-42's lists of notes are below, with them
12E1  data   fm_effect_08_pitches  u16[8]

# ======== what nothing reaches ========
# 6 bytes after the last routine before the 0xFF fill (D1 F8 54 6C 5A 5A): only the ROM check (0962) sums them,
# with every other byte; and a 1A and zeros after the fill, up to the code the reset jumps to (0923): nothing reads them
089D  fill   6
08D6  fill   77
# routines nothing calls (Unused: no call, jump or table leads to them)
358A  func   set_volume_stop_at_7f
# a group of 4 FM effects on channel 7 (the record F233) no command the game sends reaches: their handlers (2774:
# commands 3C-3F, of which 3C-3E would reach them, 01E5) and their priorities after them (277C); each handler sets the
# patch before it (2799 "LD HL,$2780; CALL $0C68")
2774  data   unused_effect_group  u16[4]
277C  data   unused_effect_priorities  u8[4]
2799  func   unused_effect_3c
280E  func   unused_effect_3d
2856  func   unused_effect_3e
289F  func   unused_effect_3f

# ======== the driver's variables ========
# (ADDRESS var NAME TYPE: in the Z80's RAM; the 12 channel records are F003-F3C2, 50h bytes each)
# a command and its argument sound_command takes instead of the ports' when not 0 (0068 "LD BC,($F000)"), and a flag
# that makes the main loop run sound_command again (0BDE): only the RAM's clearing writes them (09FB)
F000  var  held_command  u16
F002  var  command_held  u8
# the K053260's registers the program keeps a copy of, to change a voice's bits: its keys (FC28: load_patch 0D2E,
# key_on 0E92, the reset 0A45), its loop and KADPCM bits (FC2A: load_patch 0D50-0D61, the KADPCM ones only: a sample's
# kadpcm bit), its pans (FC2C-FC2D: set_pan 0FAB-0FDE, two voices each)
F3C3  var  pcm_keys  u8
F3C4  var  pcm_modes  u8
F3C5  var  pcm_pans_1  u8
F3C6  var  pcm_pans_2  u8
# the PCM voices keyed on (key_on 0EA1 sets the voice's bit, key_off 0ED6 clears it): run_pcm_channel moves only a
# free voice's volume (0BFE), key_on does nothing to a keyed one (0E8B)
F3C7  var  pcm_keyed  u8
# where next_pointer_byte reads its next byte (the reset sets 0100: 0B14)
F3C8  var  byte_pointer  u16
# the fade between songs (start_sound's C0-EF, 0458-0466; command FD, 088F): on (1), the attenuation it adds to the
# music's volume (set_music_volume 494B), every so many passes (F3CC, counted down in F3CD) one more; at 30 the next song
# starts (0BCC-0BD5), at 7F the music stops (0BC7); F3CF counts down a fade of another kind (0BA7), which nothing starts
F3CA  var  fade  u8
F3CB  var  fade_attenuation  u8
F3CC  var  fade_period  u8
F3CD  var  fade_timer  u8
F3CE  var  fade_next_song  u8
F3CF  var  fade_hold  u8
# the main loop's passes (0BEC), which spread the music's ticks (music_tick 428E, one pass in 8 a record) and the PCM
# records' volume steps (run_pcm_channel 0BF3, one pass in 16) over the records
F3D0  var  pass_count  u8
# counted up by command F8 (088A), read by nothing
F3D1  var  command_f8_count  u8

# ======== the channel records ========
# The 12 channel records (F003-F3C2, 50h bytes each): F003-F233 the YM2151's channels 0-7, F283-F373 the K053260's
# voices 0-3 (sound_main 0A4D-0AF4 "ld ix,$F003; ld (ix+$40),$00" up to "ld ix,$F373; ld (ix+$40),$0B"); the C names
# each by its chip's channel (game_profile's SOUND_NAMED_BASES: CHANNEL(FM_7)->sound). The main loop runs every one
# through run_channel (0B47-0B98, "ld ix,$F003; call $0C18"...): the song's track it plays (+10-+3F, music_tick) and
# the handler it keeps (+43): an effect's or a drum's, whose own bytes are +00-+07 (each family's scoped lines say
# what its handlers keep there; the fields below are what most of them keep), or the music's note. +40-+4F are the
# chip's: what the YM2151's channel was loaded with, or (scoped channel pcm) where the K053260's voice is.
struct  channel 50
array   channel F003 12
# fm_effect_02 10D7 "ld (ix+$00),$30", 10E1 "dec (ix+$00); ret nz": passes before the effect goes on; fm_effect_05 1212
# "ld (ix+$00),$04", 125C "dec (ix+$00); jp nz,$1216": the times a step plays
field   channel 00 count u8
# fm_effect_01 1131-114E "ld (ix+$01),$20", fm_effect_01_move_pitch 1160 "dec (ix+$01)": the passes left of a step
field   channel 01 timer u8
# fm_effect_05_retrigger 1219 "ld (ix+$02),l; ld (ix+$03),h" then "call $0DE8" (set_pitch); fm_effect_05_slide 122F-123C
# "ld l,(ix+$02); ld h,(ix+$03); ld de,$0010; add hl,de; ld (ix+$02),l; ld (ix+$03),h": the pitch the effect moves
field   channel 02 pitch u16
# fm_effect_2c 2206 "ld (ix+$04),$05", fm_effect_2c_step_down 225B "dec (ix+$04); jp nz,$220A": the times the whole
# sequence of steps plays (fm_effect_30 2497, 24DF; and seven more)
field   channel 04 rounds u8
# only FM kit 2's drum 4 uses the bytes after it (fm_drum_2_4 4D6F-4D81 "ld (ix+$05),h ... ld (ix+$06),l; ld (ix+$07),h":
# its family's scoped lines say what they hold there, the high byte of its pitch and a word of passes)
field   channel 05 extra_byte u8
field   channel 06 extra_word u16
# The music's: start_music_track (41AE-4268) clears +11-+39 and sets the rest; music_tick (4289) reads the track
# 41AE "ld (ix+$10),a" (start_music_channel's: the song's command, 80-AF); 4289 music_tick "ld a,(ix+$10); or a; ret
# z": the track plays while it is set; stop_music_channel 4278 and the track's FF (48C9) clear it
field   channel 10 song u8
# 48B6-48C3 music_tick (command FE) "ld (ix+$11),a ... ld (ix+$42),a"; 42C5 "ld a,(ix+$11); cp (ix+$42); ret c": the
# music's priority, which takes the record's sound over when it is not lower
field   channel 11 music_priority u8
# 46A6-46C9 music_tick (command E0) "ld (ix+$12),a", then 494C (FM) or 4E4E (PCM) plus 32 times it into +28; 42CC "ld
# a,(ix+$12); or a; jp nz,$45F5": not 0, a note runs a drum routine of the kit instead
field   channel 12 drum_kit u8
# 46D2 music_tick (command E1) "ld (ix+$13),a"; 42A1 "ld e,(ix+$13) ... add hl,de": units added to time each turn
field   channel 13 tempo u8
# 472E music_tick (command E4) "ld (ix+$14),a"; set_note_gate 4919 "ld a,(ix+$14)": the part (of 256) of a note keyed on
field   channel 14 gate u8
# 4649-4653 music_tick (commands 01-0F) "ld (ix+$15),a" (the octave times 24); 4310 "add a,(ix+$15)": the bytes into the
# pitch tables
field   channel 15 octave u8
# 4767 music_tick (command E6) "ld (ix+$16),a"; 4321 "ld l,(ix+$16); ld h,$00; add hl,de": added to the note's pitch
field   channel 16 detune u8
# 471E music_tick (command E3) "ld (ix+$17),a"; update_music_volume 4947 "ld a,(ix+$17)": the music's volume on the record
field   channel 17 music_volume u8
# 47DC music_tick (command EF) "ld (ix+$18),a"; 42EA, 4712 "ld a,(ix+$18); call $0F7C" (set_pan)
field   channel 18 music_pan u8
# 4701 music_tick (command E2) "ld (ix+$19),e; ld (ix+$1a),d" from music_fm_patches or music_pcm_patches; 42DB "ld
# l,(ix+$19); ld h,(ix+$1a); call $0C68" (load_patch): the instrument's patch
field   channel 19 patch u16
# 47D3 music_tick (command EE) "ld (ix+$1b),a"; music_note_tick 447E "ld a,(ix+$1b); ld (ix+$2e),a": passes between
# the steps of the LFO's sensitivity after a note starts (0: none)
field   channel 1B lfo_ramp u8
# The pitch envelope (command E7, 4770-47A3: +24, then +1C-+23 a byte each): a note starts its first segment (434C-4355
# "ld a,(ix+$24); ld (ix+$2a),a; ld a,(ix+$1c); ld (ix+$2b),a"); each segment adds its step to the pitch every period
# passes, its count times (music_pitch_envelope_1 437C "ld e,(ix+$1d)", 439B "ld a,(ix+$1e); ld (ix+$2b),a" ...)
field   channel 1C envelope_count_1 u8
field   channel 1D envelope_step_1 s8
field   channel 1E envelope_count_2 u8
field   channel 1F envelope_step_2 s8
field   channel 20 envelope_count_3 u8
field   channel 21 envelope_step_3 s8
field   channel 22 envelope_count_4 u8
field   channel 23 envelope_step_4 s8
field   channel 24 envelope_period u8
# 4201 start_music_track "ld (ix+$25),$00": nothing else reads or writes it
field   channel 25 spare u8
# 46F4 music_tick (command E2 on a PCM record) "ld (ix+$26),e; ld (ix+$27),d" from music_pcm_transpositions; 4317 "ld
# e,(ix+$26); ld d,(ix+$27); add hl,de": bytes into the pitch table
field   channel 26 transposition s16
# 46C9 music_tick (command E0) "ld (ix+$28),l; ld (ix+$29),h"; 4604 "ld l,(ix+$28); ld h,(ix+$29); add hl,de" then the
# routine there into the handler: the kit's 16 drum routines (instrument_routines)
field   channel 28 drum_routines u16
# 434F "ld (ix+$2a),a" (the period); music_pitch_envelope_1 4372 "dec (ix+$2a); ret nz": passes to the next step
field   channel 2A envelope_timer u8
# 4355 "ld (ix+$2b),a"; music_pitch_envelope_1 4397 "dec (ix+$2b); ret nz": steps left of the segment
field   channel 2B envelope_count u8
# 4327 music_tick "ld (ix+$2c),l; ld (ix+$2d),h" then set_pitch; music_pitch_envelope_1 4387-4391 adds the step to it
field   channel 2C note_pitch u16
# 433A "ld (ix+$2e),a" (lfo_ramp); music_note_tick 445F "dec (ix+$2e)": passes to the LFO sensitivity's next step
field   channel 2E lfo_ramp_timer u8
# 433D "ld (ix+$2f),$00"; music_note_tick 4465-4479 "inc (ix+$2f); ld a,(ix+$2f); call $0F3C ... cp $07": the
# sensitivity's step, up to 7
field   channel 2F lfo_ramp_step u8
# 47FC music_tick (command F1) "inc (ix+$30) ... cp (ix+$30)": the times the command was read, up to its count
field   channel 30 jump_count u8
# 4818 music_tick (command F2) "ld (ix+$31),a", 482D (F3) "dec (ix+$31)"; 481E-4827 the track's place into +32
field   channel 31 loop_count u8
field   channel 32 loop_start u16
# 4842 (command F4) "ld (ix+$34),a", 4857 (F5) "dec (ix+$34)"; 4848-4851 the track's place into +35
field   channel 34 loop_2_count u8
field   channel 35 loop_2_start u16
# 486C (command F6) "ld (ix+$37),a; ... ld (ix+$38),l; ld (ix+$39),h", 488E-48A1 (F7) "ld l,(ix+$38) ... dec (ix+$37)":
# a part played its count of times, and where the track goes on after it
field   channel 37 part_count u8
field   channel 38 part_return u16
# 4255 start_music_track "ld (ix+$3a),$FF; ld (ix+$3b),$FF"; 429B-42AD "ld l,(ix+$3a) ... add hl,de; ld (ix+$3a),l;
# ld (ix+$3b),h; ret nc": the tempo added each turn, the next step read at its carry; add_note_length 48EC subtracts
# a note's length (note_lengths are negative)
field   channel 3A time u16
# set_note_gate 4940 "ld (ix+$3c),l; ld (ix+$3d),h"; music_note_tick 449A "ld e,(ix+$3c) ... add hl,de; ret nc; jp
# $0EBE": the note is keyed off when time is that close to its end
field   channel 3C gate_time u16
# 4265 start_music_track "ld (ix+$3e),e; ld (ix+$3f),d"; next_track_byte 4957-4962: the place of the track's next byte
field   channel 3E track u16
# The chip's: sound_main 0A51 "ld (ix+$40),$00" ... 0AF4 "ld (ix+$40),$0B"; write_fm_channel_register 0C3A "ld
# a,(ix+$40); add a,b": the YM2151's channel 0-7, or 8-B the K053260's voice 0-3
field   channel 40 chip_channel u8
# start_sound 01B5 "ld (ix+$41),c"; 4358 music_tick "ld a,(ix+$10); ld (ix+$41),a": the sound it plays (an effect, or
# the music's song: bit 7); stop_channel 0C28 "ld (ix+$41),$00"
field   channel 41 sound u8
# start_sound 01B1 "cp (ix+$42); ret c", 01B8 "ld (ix+$42),a": the priority of what it plays (effect_priorities,
# music_priority); stop_channel_unless_kept 016B "ld a,(ix+$42); cp $C0"
field   channel 42 priority u8
# set_channel_handler 0C33 "ld (ix+$43),l; ld (ix+$44),h"; run_channel 0C1B "ld l,(ix+$43); ld h,(ix+$44); jp (hl)":
# what runs the record each pass
field   channel 43 handler u16
# set_volume 0D72 "ld (ix+$45),a"; write_operator_level 0DAC "add a,(ix+$45)" (the carriers' total levels: 0 loudest,
# 7F silent), 0DC0 "ld a,(ix+$45)" (the K053260's volume is 7F less it)
field   channel 45 volume u8
# What the YM2151's channel was loaded with (load_patch 0C76-0D23, its patch's bytes between the register writes): the
# connection (register 20: 0C77 "ld (ix+$46),c"; update_volume 0D7D "and $07": the algorithm; set_pan 0F89-0F8F its
# bits 6-7), the operators' total levels (0C88, 0CB3, 0CDE, 0D09; update_volume 0D8A-0DA2 writes them to registers 60,
# 70, 68, 78 with the volume), their release rates (registers E0, F0, E8, F8: 0CA2, 0CCD, 0CF8, 0D23; restore_release puts
# them back) and the LFO's sensitivities (register 38: set_channel_pm_sensitivity 0F49-0F4F, bits 4-6 PMS, 0-1 AMS)
field   channel 46 connection u8
field   channel 47 m1_total_level u8
field   channel 48 c1_total_level u8
field   channel 49 m2_total_level u8
field   channel 4A c2_total_level u8
field   channel 4B m1_release u8
field   channel 4C c1_release u8
field   channel 4D m2_release u8
field   channel 4E c2_release u8
field   channel 4F lfo_sensitivity u8
# What a K053260 voice's record keeps there instead (the pcm_ functions, and run_pcm_channel's): pcm_load_patch 0D66
# "ld (ix+$46),a" (the patch's level), pcm_update_volume 0DC3 "add a,(ix+$46)"
scoped  channel pcm 46 level u8
# pcm_load_patch 0D6C "ld (ix+$47),a"; run_pcm_channel 0C07-0C12 "ld a,(ix+$48); add a,(ix+$47) ... ld (ix+$48),a":
# how much quieter the voice gets every 16 passes while it is not keyed
scoped  channel pcm 47 decay_step u8
# pcm_key_on 0EB7 "ld (ix+$48),$00", pcm_fast_release 0F01 "ld (ix+$48),$7F"; pcm_update_volume 0DCB "add a,(ix+$48)":
# the attenuation the decay has reached
scoped  channel pcm 48 decay u8
# sound_main 0A95-0A9B "ld hl,$FC08; ld (ix+$4a),l; ld (ix+$4b),h" (FC10, FC18, FC20 for the others); pcm_set_pitch
# 0E71 "ld l,(ix+$4a); ld h,(ix+$4b); ld (hl),e": where the voice's 8 registers are (pitch, length, start, volume at +7)
scoped  channel pcm 4A registers u16
# sound_main 0A9E "ld (ix+$4c),$FE", 0AA2 "ld (ix+$4d),$01" (FD 02, FB 04, F7 08 for the others): the voice's bit of the
# K053260's key register (FC28) and the mask that clears it (pcm_key_on 0E8B-0EAD, pcm_key_off 0ECC-0ED9)
scoped  channel pcm 4C key_mask u8
scoped  channel pcm 4D key_bit u8
# sound_main 0AA6 "ld (ix+$4e),$EF", 0AAA "ld (ix+$4f),$10" (DF 20, BF 40, 7F 80): the voice's KADPCM bit of FC2A
# (pcm_modes; bits 4-7 of the K053260's register 2A, MAME's k053260.cpp) and the mask that clears it (pcm_load_patch
# 0D50-0D5B: set when the sample's kadpcm bit is)
scoped  channel pcm 4E kadpcm_mask u8
scoped  channel pcm 4F kadpcm_bit u8
# run_pcm_channel only runs on the K053260's records (sound_main_loop 0B7F-0B98): what it reads through the record
# are the pcm family's fields
param   run_pcm_channel ix channel channel.pcm

# ======== what the driver's routines take ========
# start_sound 00A0 "cp $80; jp nc,$034E" (and 0455, 0BD5 "ld a,($F3CE)"): the song's command, 80-AF
param   start_song a song
# start_song 0369 "ex de,hl" (the header's address), then 03D0-03D3 "ld e,(hl); inc hl; ld d,(hl); inc hl": the
# header's word for this record's track, HL past it for the next record
param   start_music_channel hl header_entry
# write_fm_channel_register 0C3A-0C3E "ld a,(ix+$40); add a,b; ld b,a"; write_fm_register 0C46 "ld a,b; ld ($F800),a",
# 0C51 "ld a,c; ld ($F801),a": B the YM2151's register, C its value
param   write_fm_channel_register bc fm_write
param   write_fm_register bc fm_write
# 0C76 load_patch "ld c,(hl)" and on (fm_patch); pcm_load_patch 0D3B "ld l,(hl)" (pcm_patch); the callers' "ld hl,$NNNN;
# call $0C68" (fm_effect_01 10E5 "ld hl,$10BE"), music_tick 42DB "ld l,(ix+$19); ld h,(ix+$1a)"
param   load_patch hl patch fm_patch
param   pcm_load_patch hl patch pcm_patch
# update_volume 0D85-0D89 "ld hl,$0DB8; add hl,bc; ld l,(hl)" (carrier_masks), 0D8A "ld a,(ix+$47); ld b,$60; call
# $0DA7"; write_operator_level 0DA7 "sla l; jp nc", 0DAC "add a,(ix+$45)", 0DB4 "ld c,a; jp $0C3A": the carriers'
# mask shifted a bit per operator, the operator's total level, its register
param   write_operator_level hl carriers
param   write_operator_level a level
param   write_operator_level bc fm_write
# set_pitch 0DF0-0E0D "ld a,h; and $1F ... ld c,l; ld b,$30" (key fraction) and the key code of note_codes;
# pcm_set_pitch 0E70-0E79 "ex de,hl; ... ld (hl),e; inc hl; ld (hl),d" (the K053260 voice's pitch registers)
param   set_pitch hl pitch
param   pcm_set_pitch hl pitch
# nothing calls restore_release: its IX is a record as everywhere else (0F06 "ld a,(ix+$40)")
param   restore_release ix channel channel
# set_lfo_pm_depth 0F2C "or $80; ld c,a; ld b,$19"; set_lfo_am_depth 0F34 "and $7F" (YM2151 register 19: PMD with
# bit 7, AMD without); music_tick 47C1, 47C7 (command ED)
param   set_lfo_pm_depth a depth
param   set_lfo_am_depth a depth
# set_channel_am_sensitivity 0F58 "and $03"; set_channel_pm_sensitivity 0F3C "and $07; rlca x4" (register 38)
param   set_channel_am_sensitivity a sensitivity
param   set_channel_pm_sensitivity a sensitivity
# set_lfo_frequency 0F70 "ld c,a; ld b,$18"; set_lfo_waveform 0F76 "ld c,a; ld b,$1B"
param   set_lfo_frequency a frequency
param   set_lfo_waveform a waveform
# set_pan 0F7C "ld c,a", 0F85 "rrc c; rrc c" (an FM channel's bits 6-7 of register 20: 1 left, 2 right, 3 both),
# 0FA5 "sla c x3" (a K053260 voice's pan 1-7)
param   set_pan a pan
# add_note_length 48DC "ld a,c; and $F0; rrca x3": the track's byte, its high nibble the length's code
param   add_note_length bc step
# the walks over the records that look for the effect's own or the one of the lowest priority (start_sound 0178-01A7
# FM, 028C-02BB PCM; sound_command 022D-025C command 3F, 02E7-031B command 7F): "ld ix,$F003; ld de,$0050; ld b,$08;
# cp (ix+$41) ... add ix,de; djnz", "push ix; pop hl" the lowest so far, "push hl; pop ix": a record each time
local   start_sound 0178,0187,018B,01A2,01A7,028C,029B,029F,02B6,02BB ix channel channel
local   sound_command 022D,023C,0240,0257,025C,02E7,02F6,02FF,0316,031B ix channel channel
# self_test_failed 0B28 "ld bc,$1000; dec bc; ld a,b; or c; jr nz": a count of the delay, no address
constant self_test_failed 1000 FAILED_DELAY
# nothing calls noise_on: 0FF2 "or $80; ld c,a ... ld b,$0F": A the noise's frequency (register 0F, bit 7 the noise on)
param   noise_on a noise_frequency
# nothing calls next_pointer_byte: 1010 "ld a,(hl)" the byte at byte_pointer, which it hands back
result  next_pointer_byte a value

# ======== the tables the C reads by name ========
# (ADDRESS table NAME TYPE: the game loads the asset piece of that name.) Some are indexed by the sound's number from
# well before their first element: the priorities of the effects 40-7F (02C0 "LD HL,$3630; ADD HL,DE", 0320) and
# their handlers (02D3, 0333 "LD HL,$3570"), the unused group 3C-3E's priorities (01F0 "LD HL,$2740") and handlers
# (0205 "LD HL,$26FC"), the songs' banks (0355 "LD DE,$5D92") and headers (0362 "LD DE,$5CB2"): their bases are tables
# of their own, by the sound (the elements before the sounds' first are the code and data there, never read). An FM
# channel's pitches are indexed by the note's number from 13 (42FE "LD HL,$448B"), the same way
3630  table  effect_priorities_40_by_sound  u8
3570  table  effect_handlers_40_by_sound  u16
2740  table  unused_effect_priorities_by_sound  u8
26FC  table  unused_effect_handlers_by_sound  u16
5D92  table  song_banks_by_sound  u8
5CB2  table  song_headers_by_sound  u16
448B  table  fm_pitches_by_note  u16
# the PCM patch pcm_drum_b_4 hands load_patch (5CB3 "LD HL,$5CB0"), 3 bytes just before the code (and two bytes
# before song_headers_by_sound, whose base it would read as without a name of its own)
5CB0  table  pcm_drum_b_4_patch  pcm_patch
# fm_effect_01 10E5 "ld hl,$10BE; call $0C68": the patch just before it
10BE  table  fm_effect_01_patch  fm_patch
1016  table  effect_handlers  u16
1086  table  effect_priorities  u8
2774  table  unused_effect_group  u16
277C  table  unused_effect_priorities  u8
2E01  table  effect_handlers_2  u16
2E79  table  effect_priorities_2  u8
3670  table  effect_priorities_40  u8
0DB8  table  carrier_masks  u8
0E10  table  note_codes  u8
4168  table  effect_levels  u16
44A5  table  fm_pitches  u16
4533  table  pcm_pitches  u16
48FA  table  note_lengths  u16
5E12  table  song_banks  u8
5E42  table  music_fm_patches  u16
63F4  table  music_pcm_patches  u16
642C  table  music_pcm_transpositions  s16
28E9  table  samples  u8
# what the code reads through a pointer it worked out, by the instruction that sets the pointer: an entry of
# music_fm_patches or music_pcm_patches (music_tick 46FD, by the channel's kind: the patch's address), of
# fm_pitches_by_note or pcm_pitches (431D), of music_commands (4660, the command's handler: read in the block the
# other ways into the switch join), of song_headers_by_sound (start_song 0365, by the song the stack kept), a sample's
# record (pcm_load_patch 0D44) and an effect's level (41A4)
elements patch_address u16
elements pitch u16
elements music_command_handler u16
elements song_header_address u16
local music_tick 46FD hl patch_entry patch_address
local music_tick 431D hl pitch_entry pitch
local music_tick 4660 hl command_entry music_command_handler
local start_song 0365 hl header_entry song_header_address
local pcm_load_patch 0D44 hl sample_entry sample
local pcm_effect_7f_set_volume 41A4 hl level effect_level

# ======== the music's functions ========
41AE  file  music  # the music: a song's tracks, their notes and commands, the pitch envelope, the gate, the fade's volume
41AE  func  start_music_track
comment start_music_track Starts the record's track: the song's command in song, the music's state cleared (tempo 1, time FFFF so that the first pass reads, gate_time 1), the track's place; a music sound playing on the record is stopped.
4278  func  stop_music_channel
comment stop_music_channel No more track on the record (song 0); a music sound playing on it is stopped.
4284  func  music_channel_song
comment music_channel_song Whether the record plays a track: its song.
4289  func  music_tick
comment music_tick The record's music, on one pass in 8 (by its chip_channel): the tempo added to time, and at its carry the track's next steps up to one that takes time: a note (its length; unless a sound of a higher priority has the record, the patch, volume and pan when the music did not have it, the gate, the pitch of fm_pitches_by_note or pcm_pitches by the note, octave, transposition and detune, key on but for a legato note, the pitch envelope and the LFO ramp from their start, the record's sound and priority the music's and music_pitch_envelope_1 its handler), a drum (the kit's routine for the note as the handler), a rest, an octave, or a command E0-FF (music_commands).
# music_tick's track commands E0-FF, where music_commands sends each (4659-4665 "ld l,c; ld h,$00; add hl,hl; ld
# de,$44A6; add hl,de ... jp (hl)"; the table's words from E0 on): the switch's cases by name
46A6  code  track_set_drum_kit          # E0: drum_kit, and drum_routines from it
46D2  code  track_set_tempo             # E1: tempo
46DB  code  track_set_instrument        # E2: patch (and transposition on the K053260), loaded at once if the music has the record
471E  code  track_set_volume            # E3: music_volume
472E  code  track_set_gate              # E4: gate
4737  code  track_held_note             # E5: a note and the lengths after it
4767  code  track_set_detune            # E6: detune
4770  code  track_set_pitch_envelope    # E7: envelope_period and the four segments' counts and steps
47A9  code  track_reset_e8              # E8: jp $0000, the reset (no track has it); E9-EC the same
47AC  code  track_reset_e9
47AF  code  track_reset_ea
47B2  code  track_reset_eb
47B5  code  track_reset_ec
47B8  code  track_set_lfo               # ED: the YM2151's LFO frequency, depths and waveform
47D3  code  track_set_lfo_ramp          # EE: lfo_ramp
47DC  code  track_set_pan               # EF: music_pan
47EC  code  track_jump                  # F0: track from the next two bytes
47FC  code  track_jump_counted          # F1: the jump on the count's read (jump_count)
4818  code  track_loop_start            # F2: loop_count, loop_start
482D  code  track_loop_end              # F3
4842  code  track_loop_2_start          # F4: loop_2_count, loop_2_start
4857  code  track_loop_2_end            # F5
486C  code  track_call_part             # F6: part_count, part_return
488E  code  track_part_return           # F7
48B0  code  track_reset_f8              # F8-FC: jp $0000, the reset (no track has them)
48B3  code  track_reset_fd              # FD: the same
48B6  code  track_set_priority          # FE: music_priority (and the record's priority while the music has it)
48C9  code  track_end                   # FF: song 0, the record's sound and priority cleared if the music had it
436A  func  music_pitch_envelope_1
comment music_pitch_envelope_1 A music note's handler while the pitch envelope's first segment runs: music_note_tick, then every envelope_period passes envelope_step_1 added to note_pitch, envelope_count_1 times; then the second segment.
43A7  func  music_pitch_envelope_2
comment music_pitch_envelope_2 The second segment: music_note_tick, then every envelope_period passes envelope_step_2 added to note_pitch, envelope_count_2 times; then the third.
43E4  func  music_pitch_envelope_3
comment music_pitch_envelope_3 The third segment: music_note_tick, then every envelope_period passes envelope_step_3 added to note_pitch, envelope_count_3 times; then the fourth.
4421  func  music_pitch_envelope_4
comment music_pitch_envelope_4 The fourth segment: music_note_tick, then every envelope_period passes envelope_step_4 added to note_pitch, envelope_count_4 times; then music_note_tick alone.
4458  func  music_note_tick
comment music_note_tick A music note's handler: the LFO ramp (every lfo_ramp passes the channel's sensitivities a step up, to 7); on the record's own pass in 16 the fade's volume when a fade runs, and key off once the time left is no more than gate_time.
48DC  func  add_note_length
comment add_note_length Adds a step's length (note_lengths, by its high nibble: negative, so it counts time down) to time; hands back the carry, set when the step ends before the time counted so far.
4916  func  set_note_gate
comment set_note_gate The note's gate_time: gate 256ths of what is left of time, at least 1; 0 when gate is 0 (the next note legato).
4947  func  update_music_volume
comment update_music_volume set_music_volume with the record's music_volume.
494A  func  set_music_volume
comment set_music_volume The music's volume on the record: the volume given plus fade_attenuation (at most 7F), then set_volume.
4957  func  next_track_byte
comment next_track_byte The track's next byte; track moves on.
4966  func  drum_done
comment drum_done A drum routine's end: the record's handler channel_idle, the note left to ring.

# ======== the effects' and the drums' steps ========
# Every effect and every drum routine is a chain of handlers: each runs on its record one main loop pass at a time
# and sets the next one with set_channel_handler (the record's +43), until the last stops the channel (stop_channel)
# or leaves the note ringing (drum_done). The first of a chain is named by the table that starts it: fm_effect_NN
# (effect_handlers[NN], command NN), pcm_effect_NN (effect_handlers_40[NN], command NN), pcm_effect_3f_NN
# (effect_handlers_2[NN], command 3F with the argument NN), pcm_drum_K_N (instrument_routines: kit K, note N) and
# fm_drum_K_N (instrument_routines_low); the rest by the chain they belong to (the first that reaches them) and what
# each does, found from the driver routines it reaches (directly or through the chain's own helpers):
#   note        loads a patch (load_patch) and plays it;       retrigger  keys the channel off and on again (key_on)
#   wait_sample waits for the PCM voice to end (pcm_voice_playing), then ends;  slide  moves the pitch a step at a time
#   pitch       sets a pitch;  transpose  moves the kept pitch without setting it;  fade  changes the volume
#   modulate    the pan or the LFO;  hold  counts down, then ends the sound;  end  ends the sound
#   delay       counts down, then goes on;  setup  sets the record's counters and goes on to the next
#   and for a helper the chain calls: move_pitch (adds DE to the pitch and sets it), set_pitch, set_volume, play,
#   update. A second step of the same kind in one chain is NAME_2, and so on.
10D7  file  fm_effects  # the FM effects (commands 01-38), each a chain of handlers its record runs one pass at a time

2799  file  unused_effects  # a group of four FM effects no command reaches (unused_effect_group)

# ======== the FM effects (fm_effects.c) and the unused group 3C-3F (unused_effects.c): the names, families, patches and comments ========
# ======== the FM effects (fm_effects.c) and the unused group (unused_effects.c): proposed lines ========
# How the chains' numbers read (set_pitch 0DF0-0E0D "ld a,h; and $1F; sub $18 ... add hl,hl; add hl,hl; ld c,l; ld b,$30
# ... ld c,h; ld hl,$0E10; add hl,bc"): the pitch word is in 64ths of a semitone. Its top 10 bits are the note of
# note_codes (8 octaves of 12: the high byte's 5 low bits wrap at 24, 4 notes each), its low 6 bits the key fraction
# (register 30). So 40 is a semitone, 300 an octave, 600 two octaves; past the 8 octaves the note wraps to the bottom.
# The comments give pitches and attenuations as the C prints them, in hex (pitch C00, attenuation 2C), and the steps
# and counts in decimal (a quarter semitone, 24 quieter, 48 passes).
# A key on while the channel's key is still on does not start the YM2151's envelope again (ymfm keys an operator on
# when its key bit changes): the chains that key on again without a key off (the retrigger of fm_effect_05, 19, 1a,
# 23, 2c-2e, 2f, 30, 33, 34-38) go on with the sounding note at its new pitch, they do not strike it again.
# start_sound (01CC-01E2) gives every FM effect volume 0 (the loudest), keyed off, released at the fastest.

# ---- FM effect 01 (and 02: 01 after 48 passes) ----
comment fm_effect_02 Effect 01's sound after 48 passes.
comment fm_effect_02_delay Counts down the 48 passes, then starts effect 01 (fm_effect_01).
comment fm_effect_01 Loads fm_effect_01_patch and keys on pitch 600, for 42 rounds of 32 passes that climb (fm_effect_01_retrigger) and then 42 that fall (fm_effect_01_pitch).
comment fm_effect_01_retrigger The climbing half: the pitch an eighth of a semitone higher each pass; after each 32 passes it drops 3 1/4 semitones, so that each round starts 3/4 of a semitone above the last. After 42 rounds the note is keyed off and on again for the falling half (fm_effect_01_pitch).
comment fm_effect_01_pitch The falling half, the mirror of the climbing one: an eighth of a semitone lower each pass and 3 1/4 semitones up after each 32, for 42 rounds; then stop_channel.
comment fm_effect_01_move_pitch Adds the step to the pitch and sets it; counts the timer down and tells whether it ran out.
# 1108 "ld de,$0008; call $1149", 110F "ld de,$FF30", 1131 "ld de,$FFF8", 1138 "ld de,$00D0"; 1149-1156 "ld l,(ix+$02); ld
# h,(ix+$03); add hl,de; ld (ix+$02),l; ld (ix+$03),h; call $0DE8": DE is added to the pitch (the step)
param fm_effect_01_move_pitch de step

# ---- FM effect 03 (and 04: 03 after 16 passes) ----
# fm_effect_01 10E5 "ld hl,$10BE; call $0C68" and the same in each chain below: the patch load_patch reads (25 bytes,
# connection C4: algorithm 4, both outputs)
115D  table  fm_effect_03_patch  fm_patch
comment fm_effect_04 Effect 03's sound after 16 passes.
comment fm_effect_04_delay Counts down the 16 passes, then starts effect 03 (fm_effect_03).
comment fm_effect_03 Loads fm_effect_03_patch and keys on pitch F00, for 16 rounds of 32 passes (fm_effect_03_pitch).
# 11A1 "ld (ix+$04),$00": the rounds, which no handler of the chain reads (fm_effect_03_pitch counts its rounds in the
# count: 11BF "dec (ix+$00)")
comment fm_effect_03_pitch Each pass the pitch an eighth of a semitone lower; after each 32 passes it jumps 2 3/4 semitones up, so that each round starts 1 1/4 semitones below the last; after 16 rounds stop_channel.
comment fm_effect_03_move_pitch Adds the step to the pitch and sets it.
# 11AB "ld de,$FFF8; call $11C6", 11B9 "ld de,$00B0"; 11C6-11D3 "ld l,(ix+$02) ... add hl,de ... jp $0DE8"
param fm_effect_03_move_pitch de step

# ---- FM effect 05 (and 06: its notes 112 passes later, from attenuation 20) ----
# fm_effect_05_note 120C "ld hl,$11D6; call $0C68" (connection FC: algorithm 4, feedback 7)
11D6  table  fm_effect_05_patch  fm_patch
# fm_effect_05 1208 "ld (ix+$04),$18", fm_effect_06 11EF-11F4 "ld a,$20; ld (ix+$04),a; call $0D72"; fm_effect_05_slide
# 1251-1259 "ld a,(ix+$04); add a,$18; ld (ix+$04),a; call $0D72" (set_volume): the attenuation the notes step through
scoped  channel fm_effect_05 04 attenuation u8
# fm_effect_06 11EF-11F4 (above), then 1205 "jp $120C" into fm_effect_05_note: the same byte
scoped  channel fm_effect_06 04 attenuation u8
comment fm_effect_06 Effect 05's notes after 112 passes, from attenuation 20 (then 38, 50 and 68).
comment fm_effect_06_delay Counts down the 112 passes, then effect 05's notes (fm_effect_05_note).
comment fm_effect_05 Four notes, at the volume start_sound leaves (0) and then at attenuations 30, 48 and 60 (fm_effect_05_note, the attenuation set at 18).
comment fm_effect_05_note Loads fm_effect_05_patch, then the note four times (fm_effect_05_retrigger).
comment fm_effect_05_retrigger Sets pitch 800 and keys on, for 128 passes of fm_effect_05_slide.
comment fm_effect_05_slide The pitch a quarter semitone higher each pass, played 28 semitones higher in turn every 32 passes; after the 128 passes 24 quieter (set_volume) and the next time (fm_effect_05_retrigger), and stop_channel after the fourth.

# ---- FM effect 07 ----
1265  table  fm_effect_07_patch  fm_patch
# fm_effect_07 1293-1299 "ld hl,$0400; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h"; fm_effect_07_slide 12B5-12C4 "ld l,(ix+$00); ld
# h,(ix+$01); dec hl; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h; ld a,l; or h; ret nz": the passes left, a word
scoped  channel fm_effect_07 00 passes u16
comment fm_effect_07 Loads fm_effect_07_patch and keys on pitch 1800, for 1024 passes of fm_effect_07_slide.
comment fm_effect_07_slide The pitch a 64th of a semitone lower each pass (16 semitones in the 1024 passes); then stop_channel.

# ---- FM effect 08 (and 09: 08 after 12 passes) ----
12C8  table  fm_effect_08_patch  fm_patch
# fm_effect_08 1305-130B "ld hl,$12E1; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h"; fm_effect_08_retrigger 1323-1330 "ld l,(ix+$00);
# ld h,(ix+$01); ld e,(hl); inc hl; ld d,(hl); inc hl; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h", 1339 "ex de,hl; call $0DE8":
# the place of the next pitch the effect plays (kref --sound-rom-reads: 1329 reads 12E1-12EF)
scoped  channel fm_effect_08 00 next_pitch u16
# fm_effect_08 130E "ld (ix+$02),$01", fm_effect_08_retrigger 1318 "dec (ix+$02); ret nz", 131F "ld (ix+$02),$18",
# fm_effect_08_hold 134A "dec (ix+$02)": the passes left of a note, one byte
scoped  channel fm_effect_08 02 timer u8
# fm_effect_09 12F1 "ld (ix+$02),$0C", fm_effect_09_delay 12FB "dec (ix+$02); ret nz", then fm_effect_08: the same byte
scoped  channel fm_effect_09 02 timer u8
# the pitches fm_effect_08_retrigger plays (1305 "ld hl,$12E1", 1329-132B "ld e,(hl); inc hl; ld d,(hl)"): 0BC0 0D80
# 0E80 0EC0 0D80 0EC0 0FC0, and FFFF, which ends them (1333 "ld a,d; cp $FF"); one effect steps through them, not two
comment fm_effect_09 Effect 08's notes after 12 passes.
comment fm_effect_09_delay Counts down the 12 passes (in the timer effect 08 then uses), then starts effect 08 (fm_effect_08).
comment fm_effect_08 Loads fm_effect_08_patch and plays the 7 notes of fm_effect_08_pitches from the next pass (fm_effect_08_retrigger).
comment fm_effect_08_retrigger Every 24 passes, key off and the next pitch of fm_effect_08_pitches keyed on; at the list's end (a word FFxx) the last note's release for 256 passes (fm_effect_08_hold).
comment fm_effect_08_hold Counts down the 256 passes, then stop_channel.
# 1329 "ld e,(hl)", 132B "ld d,(hl)", then 1333 "ld a,d; cp $FF" and 1339 "ex de,hl; call $0DE8": the next pitch's bytes
local fm_effect_08_retrigger 1329 e pitch_low
local fm_effect_08_retrigger 132B d pitch_high

# ---- FM effect 0C (and 0D: 0C after 16 passes) ----
1351  table  fm_effect_0c_patch  fm_patch
comment fm_effect_0d Effect 0C's notes after 16 passes.
comment fm_effect_0d_delay Counts down the 16 passes, then starts effect 0C (fm_effect_0c).
comment fm_effect_0c Loads fm_effect_0c_patch and keys on pitch 1300: six notes of 24 passes, 8 semitones down and 2 up in turn (fm_effect_0c_retrigger), the last one held.
comment fm_effect_0c_retrigger After the 24 passes, the note 8 semitones lower (fm_effect_0c_move_pitch); then fm_effect_0c_retrigger_2.
comment fm_effect_0c_retrigger_2 After the 24 passes, the note 2 semitones higher and fm_effect_0c_retrigger again; the third time the note instead goes on 256 passes more (fm_effect_0c_hold).
comment fm_effect_0c_hold Counts down the 256 passes, then stop_channel.
comment fm_effect_0c_move_pitch Keys off, moves the pitch by the step, sets it and keys on again, for 24 passes.
# 139F "ld de,$FE00; call $13D2", 13B5 "ld de,$0080"; 13D5-13E2 "ld l,(ix+$02) ... add hl,de ... call $0DE8"
param fm_effect_0c_move_pitch de step

# ---- FM effect 0E ----
13EC  table  fm_effect_0e_patch  fm_patch
# 1440-1450 "ld de,$FA00 ... add hl,de ... call $0DE8; ld (ix+$01),$40": a jump 24 semitones down that starts each of the
# rising sweeps (fm_effect_0e_pitch_3 1467 "jp nz,$1440"), no slide
comment fm_effect_0e Loads fm_effect_0e_patch and keys on pitch 1400: a dive of 4 octaves, a climb, then 8 rising sweeps of 2 octaves (fm_effect_0e_pitch).
comment fm_effect_0e_pitch The dive: a semitone lower each pass for 48 passes; then 96 passes of fm_effect_0e_pitch_2.
comment fm_effect_0e_pitch_2 The climb: 3/8 of a semitone higher each pass for the 96 passes, the first 33 played 28 semitones higher; then the first sweep (fm_effect_0e_round).
comment fm_effect_0e_round Starts a sweep: the pitch 24 semitones lower, for 64 passes of fm_effect_0e_pitch_3.
comment fm_effect_0e_pitch_3 The sweep: 3/8 of a semitone higher each pass for the 64 passes (back where it started), the first pass 28 semitones higher; after 8 sweeps (fm_effect_0e_round) stop_channel.
comment fm_effect_0e_move_pitch Adds the step to the pitch and sets it, 28 semitones higher while the timer is 64 or more; counts the timer down and tells whether it ran out.
# 1428 "ld de,$FFC0; call $146D", 1439 "ld de,$0018", 145D "ld de,$0018"; 146D-1473 "ld l,(ix+$02) ... add hl,de"
param fm_effect_0e_move_pitch de step

# ---- FM effect 0F ----
# 14A5 "ld hl,$148C; call $0C68": the same 25 bytes as fm_effect_0e_patch
148C  table  fm_effect_0f_patch  fm_patch
comment fm_effect_0f Loads fm_effect_0f_patch (fm_effect_0e_patch's bytes) and keys on pitch C00, for 8 rounds of 64 passes (fm_effect_0f_slide).
comment fm_effect_0f_slide The pitch 3/8 of a semitone higher each pass, played 16 semitones higher in turn every 16 passes; after each 64 passes it drops 16 semitones, so that each round starts 7 5/8 semitones above the last; after 8 rounds stop_channel.

# ---- FM effect 10 ----
1509  table  fm_effect_10_patch  fm_patch
comment fm_effect_10 Loads fm_effect_10_patch and keys on pitch 1000, for 8 rounds of 64 passes (fm_effect_10_slide).
comment fm_effect_10_slide The pitch a semitone higher each pass, played 4 semitones higher in turn every 8 passes; after each 64 passes it drops 16 semitones; after 8 rounds stop_channel. It climbs past the top of the 8 octaves, where set_pitch starts again from the bottom.

# ---- FM effect 11 ----
1586  table  fm_effect_11_patch  fm_patch
# fm_effect_11 15B4-15BA "ld hl,$0200; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h"; fm_effect_11_pitch 15C9-15D8 "ld l,(ix+$00) ...
# dec hl ... ld a,h; or l; ret nz": the passes left, a word; fm_effect_11_move_pitch 15E9 "bit 1,(ix+$00)" reads its
# low byte alone
scoped  channel fm_effect_11 00 passes u16
scoped  channel fm_effect_11 00 passes_low u8
comment fm_effect_11 Loads fm_effect_11_patch and keys on pitch 1700, for 512 passes of fm_effect_11_pitch.
comment fm_effect_11_pitch A trill sliding down: the pitch an eighth of a semitone lower each pass (64 semitones in the 512 passes, fm_effect_11_move_pitch); then stop_channel.
comment fm_effect_11_move_pitch Adds the step to the pitch and sets it, 4 semitones higher in turn every 2 passes (by the passes left).
# 15C3 "ld de,$FFF8; call $15DC"; 15DC-15E2 "ld l,(ix+$02); ld h,(ix+$03); add hl,de"
param fm_effect_11_move_pitch de step

# ---- FM effect 12 ----
15F7  table  fm_effect_12_patch  fm_patch
# fm_effect_12 1616-161F "ld hl,$0400; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h; call $0DE8"; fm_effect_12_slide 1651-1669 "ld
# l,(ix+$00); ld h,(ix+$01); dec hl; dec hl; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h ... call $0DE8": the pitch is at 00 here
scoped  channel fm_effect_12 00 pitch u16
# fm_effect_12 1625-1629 "ld (ix+$02),$00; ld (ix+$03),$03"; fm_effect_12_delay 1633-1642 "ld l,(ix+$02); ld
# h,(ix+$03); dec hl ... ret nz", 1643-1647 "ld (ix+$02),$A0; ld (ix+$03),$01"; fm_effect_12_slide 165F "bit
# 0,(ix+$02)", 166C-167B "dec hl ... ret nz": the passes left, a word, its low byte read alone
scoped  channel fm_effect_12 02 passes u16

comment fm_effect_2d_set_pitch Sets the pitch it is given, 28 semitones higher while bit 5 of the timer is set: a high blip over the first 17 passes of each note.
# fm_effect_2e_patch sends the channel to the right output only, and the board wires only the YM2151's left one (MAME's
# simpsons.cpp; CLAUDE.md): effect 2E is never heard. The same for fm_effect_34_patch
comment fm_effect_2e Effect 2C's figure 64 passes later, an eighth of a semitone higher, quieter and on the right output only (fm_effect_2e_patch): its own copy of the chain.
comment fm_effect_2e_note After the 64 passes, loads fm_effect_2e_patch: 5 rounds of the figure (fm_effect_2e_retrigger).
comment fm_effect_2e_retrigger Starts a round: pitch C08 keyed on, 48 passes a note (fm_effect_2e_step_up).
comment fm_effect_2e_step_up Plays the note (fm_effect_2e_play); every 48 passes the next, 3 semitones higher (fm_effect_2e_transpose), three times; then the round's last note (fm_effect_2e_step_down).
comment fm_effect_2e_step_down The round's last note, 4 semitones lower, for 48 passes; then the next round (fm_effect_2e_retrigger), and stop_channel after the fifth.
comment fm_effect_2e_transpose Moves the pitch by the step and sets it (fm_effect_2e_set_pitch).
comment fm_effect_2e_play Sets the pitch (fm_effect_2e_set_pitch).
comment fm_effect_2e_set_pitch Sets the pitch it is given, 28 semitones higher while bit 5 of the timer is set: a high blip over the first 17 passes of each note.
# fm_effect_2c_step_up 2234 "ld de,$00C0; jp nz,$2264", fm_effect_2c_step_down 2255 "ld de,$FF00"; 2264-226A "ld
# l,(ix+$02); ld h,(ix+$03); add hl,de": added to the pitch
param fm_effect_2c_transpose de step
param fm_effect_2d_transpose de step
param fm_effect_2e_transpose de step
# 2271 "jp $227A" with HL the pitch just stored (2264-226E), 2274-2277 "ld l,(ix+$02); ld h,(ix+$03)"; 227A-2285 "...
# add hl,de; jp $0DE8" (set_pitch)
param fm_effect_2c_set_pitch hl pitch
param fm_effect_2d_set_pitch hl pitch
param fm_effect_2e_set_pitch hl pitch

# ---- FM effect 2F ----
23E6  table  fm_effect_2f_patch  fm_patch
# fm_effect_2f 2409 "ld (ix+$04),$0B"; fm_effect_2f_slide 2448-2450 "ld a,(ix+$04); add a,$0B; ld (ix+$04),a; call
# $0D72" (set_volume): the attenuation
scoped  channel fm_effect_2f 04 attenuation u8
comment fm_effect_2f Loads fm_effect_2f_patch: a falling note four times (fm_effect_2f_retrigger), at the volume start_sound leaves (0) and then at attenuations 16, 21 and 2C; effect 19's figure from pitch 1400, falling slower.
comment fm_effect_2f_retrigger Sets pitch 1400 and keys on, for 64 passes of fm_effect_2f_slide.
comment fm_effect_2f_slide The pitch 3/16 of a semitone lower each pass (an octave in the 64 passes), played an octave higher in turn every 16 passes; then 11 quieter (set_volume) and the next time (fm_effect_2f_retrigger), and stop_channel after the fourth.

# ---- FM effect 30 (and 31: 30 after 80 passes; 32: 31 after 32 more; 33: 30's copy an eighth of a semitone higher) ----
245C  table  fm_effect_30_patch  fm_patch
comment fm_effect_32 Effect 30's notes after 112 passes: 32 passes, then effect 31's 80.
comment fm_effect_32_delay Counts down the 32 passes, then starts effect 31 (fm_effect_31).
comment fm_effect_31 Effect 30's notes after 80 passes.
comment fm_effect_31_delay Counts down the 80 passes, then starts effect 30 (fm_effect_30).
comment fm_effect_30 Loads fm_effect_30_patch: 4 rounds of 8 notes, each a whole tone lower (fm_effect_30_retrigger).
comment fm_effect_30_retrigger Starts a round: pitch 1200 keyed on, 8 notes of 48 passes (fm_effect_30_retrigger_2).
comment fm_effect_30_retrigger_2 Every 48 passes, key off and the next note 2 semitones lower, keyed on; after the eighth the next round (fm_effect_30_retrigger), and stop_channel after the fourth.
# fm_effect_33 2501 "ld hl,$24E8; call $0C68": the same 25 bytes as fm_effect_30_patch
24E8  table  fm_effect_33_patch  fm_patch
comment fm_effect_33 Effect 30's notes an eighth of a semitone higher (its own copy of the chain, and of the patch).
comment fm_effect_33_retrigger Starts a round: pitch 1208 keyed on, 8 notes of 48 passes (fm_effect_33_retrigger_2).
comment fm_effect_33_retrigger_2 Every 48 passes, key off and the next note 2 semitones lower, keyed on; after the eighth the next round (fm_effect_33_retrigger), and stop_channel after the fourth.

comment fm_effect_35 Effect 34's rises 32 passes later, on the left output only (fm_effect_35_patch): its own copy of the chain.
comment fm_effect_35_note After the 32 passes, loads fm_effect_35_patch: 2 rounds of 8 slow rises (fm_effect_35_setup).

comment fm_effect_35_retrigger Sets pitch C00 and keys on, for 192 passes of fm_effect_35_slide.
comment fm_effect_35_slide The pitch a 32nd of a semitone higher each pass (6 semitones in the 192 passes); then the next rise (fm_effect_35_retrigger); after the eighth, key off and the next round (fm_effect_35_setup), and stop_channel after the second.

# ---- FM effect 38 ----
# 2717 "ld hl,$26FE; call $0C68": the same 25 bytes as fm_effect_19_patch; its first two bytes are also the second
# word of unused_effect_handlers_by_sound (26FC), whose elements before 2774 nothing reads
26FE  table  fm_effect_38_patch  fm_patch
# fm_effect_38 2721 "ld (ix+$04),$08"; fm_effect_38_slide 2760-2768 "ld a,(ix+$04); add a,$08; ld (ix+$04),a; call
# $0D72" (set_volume): the attenuation
scoped  channel fm_effect_38 04 attenuation u8
comment fm_effect_38 Loads fm_effect_38_patch (fm_effect_19_patch's bytes): a falling note four times (fm_effect_38_retrigger), at the volume start_sound leaves (0) and then at attenuations 10, 18 and 20; effect 19's figure from pitch 1000, fading slower.
comment fm_effect_38_retrigger Sets pitch 1000 and keys on, for 64 passes of fm_effect_38_slide.
comment fm_effect_38_slide The pitch a quarter semitone lower each pass, played an octave higher in turn every 16 passes; after the 64 passes 8 quieter (set_volume) and the next time (fm_effect_38_retrigger), and stop_channel after the fourth.

# ======== the unused group (unused_effects.c): commands 3C-3F on FM channel 7, with the YM2151's noise ========
# start_sound 01E5-0225 takes the commands 3C-3E on the record F233 (FM_7, the only channel the YM2151's noise plays
# on) and their handlers from unused_effect_group; 3F never gets there (sound_command takes 3F first, 0083): no command
# reaches unused_effect_3f, and the game sends none of 3C-3E. Unlike the commands 01-38 (01DE), these do not turn the
# noise off first, and nothing turns it off when they end (stop_channel), until the next FM effect 01-38 starts
# unused_effect_3c 2799 "ld hl,$2780; call $0C68" and the same in the other three: the patch before each
2780  table  unused_effect_3c_patch  fm_patch
27F5  table  unused_effect_3d_patch  fm_patch
283D  table  unused_effect_3e_patch  fm_patch
2886  table  unused_effect_3f_patch  fm_patch
# nothing calls them (unused_effect_group is data to the walk): their IX is a record as everywhere else (279F-27A1 "ld
# a,$00; ld (ix+$01),a", 27B6 "ld (ix+$00),$10")
param unused_effect_3c ix channel channel
param unused_effect_3d ix channel channel
param unused_effect_3e ix channel channel
param unused_effect_3f ix channel channel
# unused_effect_3c 279F-27A4 "ld a,$00; ld (ix+$01),a; call $0FF2" (noise_on); unused_effect_3c_slide 27D0 "bit
# 0,(ix+$01)", 27E6-27F2 "ld a,(ix+$01); inc a; cp $20; jp nc,$0C22; ld (ix+$01),a; jp $0FF2": the noise's frequency
scoped  channel unused_effect_3c 01 noise_frequency u8
# unused_effect_3d 2814-2819 "ld a,$0A; ld (ix+$01),a; call $0FF2"; 2831-283A "dec (ix+$01); jp z,$0C22; ld
# a,(ix+$01); jp $0FF2": the noise's frequency
scoped  channel unused_effect_3d 01 noise_frequency u8
# unused_effect_3e 2864-286A "ld hl,$0400; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h"; unused_effect_3e_end 2873-2882 "dec hl ...
# ret nz": the passes left, a word
scoped  channel unused_effect_3e 00 passes u16
# unused_effect_3f 28AD-28B3 "ld hl,$1600 ...", 28C3-28C9 "ld hl,$0200 ..."; unused_effect_3f_update 28D9-28E7 "dec
# hl ... or h": the passes left, a word
scoped  channel unused_effect_3f 00 passes u16
# 2829-283A: every 16 passes the noise's frequency one lower (noise_on), stop_channel at 0: no hold
comment unused_effect_3c Unused: command 3C's handler (unused_effect_group). Loads unused_effect_3c_patch on FM channel 7, the noise on at frequency 0, and keys on pitch 800: 32 steps of 16 passes (unused_effect_3c_slide).
comment unused_effect_3c_slide The pitch a 16th of a semitone higher each pass, 2 semitones higher while the noise's frequency is odd; every 16 passes the noise's frequency one higher (noise_on), and stop_channel when it reaches 32.
comment unused_effect_3d Unused: command 3D's handler (unused_effect_group). Loads unused_effect_3d_patch on FM channel 7, the noise on at frequency 0A, and keys on at the pitch the channel has: unused_effect_3d_lower_noise.
comment unused_effect_3d_lower_noise Every 16 passes the noise's frequency one lower (noise_on); stop_channel when it reaches 0.
comment unused_effect_3e Unused: command 3E's handler (unused_effect_group). Loads unused_effect_3e_patch on FM channel 7, the noise on at frequency 12, and keys on at the pitch the channel has, for 1024 passes (unused_effect_3e_end).
comment unused_effect_3e_end Counts down the 1024 passes, then stop_channel.
comment unused_effect_3f Unused: command 3F's handler in unused_effect_group, which no command reaches. Loads unused_effect_3f_patch on FM channel 7, the noise on at frequency 1F, and keys on at the pitch the channel has, for 5632 passes (unused_effect_3f_step).
comment unused_effect_3f_step After the 5632 passes (unused_effect_3f_update), key off and 512 passes of unused_effect_3f_end.
comment unused_effect_3f_end Counts down the 512 passes (unused_effect_3f_update), then stop_channel.
comment unused_effect_3f_update Counts the passes down and tells whether they ran out.

# ======== the bytes the handlers split their words into ========
# The handlers that count a word of passes down ("ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,(ix+$01); dec hl; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h; ld
# a,l; or h; ret nz"): L and H its bytes. E.g. fm_effect_07_slide 12B5-12C4, fm_effect_13_slide 16E7-16F6
local fm_effect_11_pitch 15CF h passes_high
local fm_effect_12_delay 1639 h passes_high
local fm_effect_1f_end 1CC3 h passes_high
local fm_effect_21_end 1D7F l passes_low
local fm_effect_21_end 1D7F h passes_high
local unused_effect_3e_end 2879 l passes_low
local unused_effect_3e_end 2879 h passes_high
local unused_effect_3f_update 28DF l passes_low
local unused_effect_3f_update 28DF h passes_high

# ======== pitches the C would print as places ========
# A pitch an effect sets with "ld hl,$NNNN" is an address to the C when a function or a table's record holds that place:
# 1000 is pcm_voice_playing's entry, 1208 fm_effect_05's, 1400 the column 14 of fm_effect_0e_patch's record (13EC-1404),
# and the C read "pitch = pcm_voice_playing_ADDRESS" (fm_effect_10 1528, fm_effect_1f_retrigger 1CA5, fm_effect_2a
# 2179, fm_effect_38_retrigger 2725), "pitch = fm_effect_05_ADDRESS" (fm_effect_33_retrigger 250B) and, with the patches
# named, "pitch = fm_effect_0e_patch_ADDRESS + 0x14u" (fm_effect_0e 140B, fm_effect_1a_retrigger 1A32, fm_effect_1b 1A95,
# fm_effect_23_retrigger 1E16, fm_effect_29 20EA, fm_effect_2f_retrigger 240D). Each goes to set_pitch (param set_pitch
# hl pitch): a number. Named here so that the C prints a number; better would be the decompiler taking what set_pitch
# is handed as a number

# ======== the PCM effects (pcm_effects.c): the names, families, patches and comments ========
# The PCM effects (pcm_effects.c, 2EB8-41AD): proposals for sound_symbols.txt
# (an agent's proposal; every value below read from the listing and the program ROM 072-e03.6g)

# ======== renames (apply to the func lines and every other use) ========
# pcm_effect_42_retrigger 375D-378C: no key off before its key on; it reads the list's next note (pitch, length) and keys it on
# pcm_effect_42_setup_2 378F-37AF: no setup; it reads the rest's length and keys the note off
# pcm_effect_7a/7c/7e: the heuristic called every handler retrigger because each calls the helper that keys the sample off and on when it has ended (4024, 40D5, 415B); the handlers themselves fade in (3FBE "dec (ix+$00); call $0D72"), hold (3FE6 "dec hl" on the u16 at 00), fade out (4007 "inc (ix+$00); call $0D72; cp $7F")
# effect_steps_36b9 (36B9 data u16[48]) is three lists of notes, 32 bytes each: pcm_effect_40 3746 "ld hl,$36B9", pcm_effect_41 3735 "ld hl,$36D9", pcm_effect_42 3724 "ld hl,$36F9"; each five notes of three words (pitch, the passes keyed on, the passes of the rest: pcm_effect_42_next_note 3767-3779, pcm_effect_42_rest 3799-379C) and a pitch 0 (376B "ld a,e; or d; jp z,$0C22")
36B9  data   pcm_effect_40_notes  u16[16]
36D9  data   pcm_effect_41_notes  u16[16]
36F9  data   pcm_effect_42_notes  u16[16]
# no sample loops: pcm_load_patch 0D50-0D5B puts bit 0 of the sample's flags into FC2A's bits 4-7 (the pcm family's kadpcm_bit 10/20/40/80), which are the K053260's KADPCM bits (runtime/src/k053260.c REG_LOOP_KADPCM: bits 0-3 loop, 4-7 KADPCM; the flag is 1 for the 101 samples assets/sounds/samples.txt calls kadpcm); so every wait_sample ends
