# ======== families: the chains whose record bytes +00-+03 differ from count, timer, pitch ========
# effects 40, 41 and 42 (pcm_effect_40 and 41 jump into pcm_effect_42_setup with their list in HL): 3749-374C
# pcm_effect_42_setup "ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h"; pcm_effect_42_next_note 3761-3769 "ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,(ix+$01);
# ld e,(hl); inc hl; ld d,(hl)", 3780-3783 "ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h": the place of the list's next word
scoped  channel pcm_effect_42 00 notes u16
# 374F-3753 pcm_effect_42_setup "ld (ix+$02),$01; ld (ix+$03),$00"; 377A-377D, 379D-37A0 "ld (ix+$02),e; ld (ix+$03),d"
# (a note's length, a rest's); pcm_effect_42_update 37B2-37C0 "ld e,(ix+$02); ld d,(ix+$03); dec de; ...; ld a,e; or d":
# the passes left of the note or the rest
scoped  channel pcm_effect_42 02 passes u16
# 3969-396B pcm_effect_50 "ld a,$7F; ld (ix+$00),a; call $0D72"; pcm_effect_50_fade 3986-398C "dec (ix+$00); ld
# a,(ix+$00); call $0D72": the volume it fades in (set_volume's)
scoped  channel pcm_effect_50 00 attenuation u8
# 3A30 pcm_effect_54 "ld (ix+$00),$90"; pcm_effect_54_modulate 3A3A-3A3E "dec (ix+$00); ret nz; ld (ix+$00),$90": the
# passes left of a step of the pan
scoped  channel pcm_effect_54 00 timer u8
# 3A25-3A2A pcm_effect_54 "ld a,$06; ld (ix+$01),a; call $0F7C"; pcm_effect_54_modulate 3A42-3A4E "dec (ix+$01); ld
# a,(ix+$01); call $0F7C; ld a,(ix+$01); cp $02": the pan it moves
scoped  channel pcm_effect_54 01 pan u8
# 3AF9-3AFF pcm_effect_57 "ld hl,$0200; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h", 3B1B-3B21 "ld hl,$0280"; pcm_effect_57_update
# 3B4F-3B5D "ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,(ix+$01); dec hl; ...; ld a,h; or l": the passes left
scoped  channel pcm_effect_57 00 passes u16
# effects 7A, 7C and 7E, the same layout. The timer first: the u16 at 00 holds its byte, and the decompiler takes a
# family's fields in the order of these lines (decompiler/pointers.py scoped_field): 3FB4 pcm_effect_7a "ld
# (ix+$01),$01"; pcm_effect_7a_fade_in 3FC1-3FC5 "dec (ix+$01); ret nz; ld (ix+$01),$01"; pcm_effect_7a_fade_out
# 400A-400E "dec (ix+$01); ret nz; ld (ix+$01),$08" (7C: 4072-4076 "ld (ix+$01),$04", 40BB-40BF; 7E: 4141-4145): the
# passes left of a step of the fade
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7a 01 timer u8
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7c 01 timer u8
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7e 01 timer u8
# 3FD7-3FDD pcm_effect_7a_fade_in "ld hl,$0A00; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h"; pcm_effect_7a_delay 3FE9-3FF7 "ld
# l,(ix+$00); ld h,(ix+$01); dec hl; ...; ld a,l; or h" (7C: 4088-408E "ld hl,$1100", 409A-40A8; 7E: pcm_effect_7e
# 410E-4114 "ld hl,$0600", pcm_effect_7e_delay 4120-412E): the passes the sound holds
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7a 00 passes u16
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7c 00 passes u16
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7e 00 passes u16
# 3FA9-3FAE pcm_effect_7a "ld a,$60; ld (ix+$00),a; call $0D72"; pcm_effect_7a_fade_in 3FC9-3FCF "dec (ix+$00); ld
# a,(ix+$00); call $0D72"; pcm_effect_7a_delay 3FF9 "ld (ix+$00),$00"; pcm_effect_7a_fade_out 4012-401E "inc (ix+$00);
# ld a,(ix+$00); call $0D72; ld a,(ix+$00); cp $7F" (7C: 405A-405F "ld a,$40", 407A-4080, 40AA, 40C3-40CF; 7E: 4130,
# 4149-4155): the volume of the fades
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7a 00 attenuation u8
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7c 00 attenuation u8
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7e 00 attenuation u8
# (the word's stores are byte stores of constants, 3FDA-3FDD "ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h" after "ld hl,$0A00": the C
# writes them as attenuation = 0 and timer = 0x0A; z80/pairs.py joined_stores only joins the two bytes of one value, as
# it does for pcm_effect_42_setup's notes. The same makes pcm_effect_57's and pcm_effect_42's stores of passes
# AS8/AT8: of constants, 3AF9-3AFF, 3B1B-3B21, 374F-3753, or of the bytes of DE and HL whose zero test reads them
# again, 37B9-37C0, 3B56-3B5D)
# sound_command 0347-034A "ld a,($FC01); ld (ix+$00),a" (command 7F: its argument, into the record playing effect 7F or
# the one it takes); pcm_effect_7f_set_volume 4194-41A4 "ld a,(ix+$00); and $07; or a; jp z,$0C22; ld l,a; ld h,$00;
# add hl,hl; ld de,$4166; add hl,de": the position 1-7 (effect_levels: pan 7 to 1, the volume 30 at the sides, 0 at 4),
# 0 the end
scoped  channel pcm_effect_7f 00 position u8

# ======== the functions ========
# command 3F keeps its argument as the record's sound (sound_command 026A "ld (ix+$41),c"): 3F 00's is 00, a free record's, so the walk for the record already playing it (022D-0239 "cp (ix+$41)") stops at the first PCM record whose sound is 00, free or playing 3F 00: a second 3F 00 may start on another voice where any other effect starts again on its own
comment pcm_effect_3f_00 Command 3F 00: sample 16 (pcm_effect_3f_00_patch, level 0) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on; then pcm_effect_3f_00_wait_sample.
comment pcm_effect_3f_00_wait_sample When sample 16 has ended (pcm_voice_playing), stop_channel.
comment pcm_effect_3f_01 Command 3F 01: sample 17 (pcm_effect_3f_01_patch, level 0) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on; then pcm_effect_3f_01_wait_sample.
comment pcm_effect_3f_01_wait_sample When sample 17 has ended (pcm_voice_playing), stop_channel.
comment pcm_effect_3f_02 Command 3F 02: sample 18 (pcm_effect_3f_02_patch, level 0) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on; then pcm_effect_3f_02_wait_sample.

# started together: sound_test_effects' word 4002 (command 40, argument 2: start_sound for 40, 41 and 42), and they run in the same 16 scenarios; effect_priorities_40 40-42 are C0, which stop_channel_unless_kept keeps through command 00

comment pcm_effect_42_setup The list of notes of effect 40, 41 or 42 into notes, 1 pass to wait, then pcm_effect_42_next_note.
comment pcm_effect_42_next_note When the passes have run out (pcm_effect_42_update), the list's next note: its pitch (0 ends the effect: stop_channel) and its length into passes, keyed on (the sample from its start); then pcm_effect_42_rest.
# 37A9 "call $0EBE": key off only lets run_pcm_channel decay the voice; with the decay step 0 of pcm_effect_40_patch and pcm_effect_41_patch it changes nothing, and the note plays on until the sample ends or the next note restarts it
comment pcm_effect_42_rest When the note's passes have run out, the rest's length from the list into passes and key off (the voice decays by its patch's decay step every 16 passes); then pcm_effect_42_next_note.
comment pcm_effect_42_update One pass less of the note or the rest (passes); true when none is left.

# 5D and 77 differ by the patch's decay step only (sample 94, 128 passes: 8, 4)
comment pcm_effect_77 Command 77: sample 94 (pcm_effect_77_patch, level 0, decay step 4) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on for 128 passes (count); then pcm_effect_77_delay.
comment pcm_effect_77_delay When the count runs out, key off (the sample fades, 4 more attenuation every 16 passes) and a count of 256 passes; then pcm_effect_77_hold.
comment pcm_effect_77_hold After 256 passes, stop_channel, whether the sample has ended or not.
# effect_priorities_40[78] is 00; sound_test_effects lists 40-7F but 78, and no scenario starts it (02D7 never reads effect_handlers_40's entry 78)
comment pcm_effect_78 Command 78: stop_channel at once. Its priority is 0 (effect_priorities_40), so it only takes a free record: an empty slot, which the sound test leaves out (sound_test_effects).
# 55 and 79 differ by the pitch only (sample 68: E40, its own)
comment pcm_effect_79 Command 79: sample 68 (pcm_effect_79_patch, level 0) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on; then pcm_effect_79_wait_sample.
comment pcm_effect_79_wait_sample When sample 68 has ended (pcm_voice_playing), stop_channel.
comment pcm_effect_7a Command 7A: sample 88 (pcm_effect_7a_patch, level 20) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on at attenuation 60 (set_volume), timer 1; then pcm_effect_7a_fade_in.
comment pcm_effect_7a_fade_in Fades in: every pass the attenuation one less (set_volume), down to 0 (96 passes), the sample played again whenever it ends (pcm_effect_7a_replay); then passes 2560 (the word over the attenuation and the timer) and pcm_effect_7a_delay.
comment pcm_effect_7a_delay Holds the sound until passes runs out, played again whenever it ends; then attenuation 0, timer 1 and pcm_effect_7a_fade_out.
comment pcm_effect_7a_fade_out Fades out: every 8 passes the attenuation one more (set_volume), the sample played again whenever it ends; at 7F, stop_channel.
comment pcm_effect_7a_replay When the sample has ended (pcm_voice_playing), key off and on: the sample again from its start.
comment pcm_effect_7b Command 7B: sample 71 (pcm_effect_7b_patch, level 0) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on; then pcm_effect_7b_wait_sample.
comment pcm_effect_7b_wait_sample When sample 71 has ended (pcm_voice_playing), stop_channel.
comment pcm_effect_7c Command 7C: sample 82 (pcm_effect_7c_patch, level 40) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on at attenuation 40 (set_volume), timer 1; then pcm_effect_7c_fade_in.
comment pcm_effect_7c_fade_in Fades in: every 4 passes the attenuation one less (set_volume), down to 0 (256 passes), the sample played again whenever it ends (pcm_effect_7c_replay); then passes 4352 (the word over the attenuation and the timer) and pcm_effect_7c_delay.
comment pcm_effect_7c_delay Holds the sound until passes runs out, played again whenever it ends; then attenuation 0, timer 1 and pcm_effect_7c_fade_out.
comment pcm_effect_7c_fade_out Fades out: every 8 passes the attenuation one more (set_volume), the sample played again whenever it ends; at 7F, stop_channel.
comment pcm_effect_7c_replay When the sample has ended (pcm_voice_playing), key off and on: the sample again from its start.
comment pcm_effect_7d Command 7D: sample 99 (pcm_effect_7d_patch, level 0) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on; then pcm_effect_7d_wait_sample.
comment pcm_effect_7d_wait_sample When sample 99 has ended (pcm_voice_playing), stop_channel.
# 7E and 7F play the same sample (89), 7E at level 50, 7F at level 30; both only in stage5
comment pcm_effect_7e Command 7E: sample 89 (pcm_effect_7e_patch, level 50) at its own pitch, pan 4 (centred), keyed on, passes 1536 (the word over the attenuation and the timer); then pcm_effect_7e_delay.
comment pcm_effect_7e_delay Holds the sound until passes runs out, the sample played again whenever it ends (pcm_effect_7e_replay); then attenuation 0, timer 1 and pcm_effect_7e_fade_out.
comment pcm_effect_7e_fade_out Fades out: every 8 passes the attenuation one more (set_volume), the sample played again whenever it ends; at 7F, stop_channel.
comment pcm_effect_7e_replay When the sample has ended (pcm_voice_playing), key off and on: the sample again from its start.
comment pcm_effect_7f Command 7F: sample 89 (pcm_effect_7f_patch, level 30) at its own pitch, its pan and volume by the position the command hands (pcm_effect_7f_set_volume), keyed on; then pcm_effect_7f_retrigger.
comment pcm_effect_7f_retrigger Every pass: when the sample has ended (pcm_voice_playing), key off and on (the sample again: it plays until position 0); then pcm_effect_7f_set_volume.
# effect_levels' entry 0 (4166-4167) would be pcm_effect_7f_patch's last two bytes: never read (position 0 stops the channel first, 419A)
comment pcm_effect_7f_set_volume The record's position (its low 3 bits; a command 7F while the effect plays changes it): 0 stops the channel, 1-7 set the pan and the volume of effect_levels (from the right, pan 7, to the left, pan 1, loudest at 4 in the centre).

# ======== parameters and locals ========
# nothing calls set_volume_stop_at_7f: its IX is a record as everywhere else (358D "ld a,(ix+$00); cp $7F", the
# attenuation the fade outs of pcm_effect_7a, 7c and 7e compare with 7F)
param   set_volume_stop_at_7f ix channel channel.pcm_effect_7a
# pcm_effect_7f_set_volume 4194-4199 "ld a,(ix+$00); and $07; or a": the position, then 419D "ld l,a" its entry
local   pcm_effect_7f_set_volume 4197 a position
# pcm_effect_42_next_note 3761-376A "ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,(ix+$01); ld e,(hl); inc hl; ld d,(hl); inc hl": the place
# of the note's pitch in the list, and the word's two bytes (the pitch; then 3776-3778, the note's length)
local   pcm_effect_42_next_note 3764,3768,376A hl note
local   pcm_effect_42_next_note 3767,3776 e word_low
local   pcm_effect_42_next_note 3769,3778 d word_high
# 3770 "push hl" around set_pitch: the place of the note's length
local   pcm_effect_42_next_note 3770 s2 saved_note
# pcm_effect_42_rest 3799-379B "ld e,(hl); inc hl; ld d,(hl)": the rest's length
local   pcm_effect_42_rest 3799 e word_low
local   pcm_effect_42_rest 379B d word_high
# pcm_effect_42_update 37B8 "dec de", then "ld (ix+$02),e; ld (ix+$03),d; ld a,e; or d": the passes left
local   pcm_effect_42_update 37B8 e passes_low
local   pcm_effect_42_update 37B8 d passes_high
# pcm_effect_57_update 3B55 "dec hl", then "ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h; ld a,h; or l"; the same in the delays of
# 7A, 7C and 7E (3FEF, 40A0, 4126 "dec hl"): the passes left, not the attenuation and the timer whose bytes they are
local   pcm_effect_57_update 3B55 l passes_low
local   pcm_effect_57_update 3B55 h passes_high
local   pcm_effect_7a_delay 3FEF l passes_low
local   pcm_effect_7a_delay 3FEF h passes_high
local   pcm_effect_7c_delay 40A0 l passes_low
local   pcm_effect_7c_delay 40A0 h passes_high
local   pcm_effect_7e_delay 4126 l passes_low
local   pcm_effect_7e_delay 4126 h passes_high
# pcm_effect_7f_set_volume 419D-41A4 "ld l,a; ld h,$00; add hl,hl; ld de,$4166; add hl,de": effect_levels by the
# position from 1 (its entry 0, 4166, would be the last two bytes of pcm_effect_7f_patch, which without this base
# names it: "pcm_effect_7f_patch_ADDRESS + 0x1u")
4166  table  effect_levels_by_position  u16

# ======== the drum routines (drums.c): the names, families, patches and comments ========
# ======== proposal: the drum routines (drums.c, fm_drum_1_1 49C5 to pcm_drum_b_f 5DA1) ========
# How they run: a track's E0 sets the record's drum_kit (music_tick 46A6-46C9: drum_routines = 494C + 32 x kit on an FM
# record, 4E4E + 32 x kit on a PCM one); then a note's low nibble N (45F5-4632) puts the kit's routine N in the record's
# handler, after key_off, set_music_volume, noise_off and the LFO sensitivities at 0. The routine runs at once (run_channel
# 0C18-0C21 reads the handler after music_tick), then every pass, until it leaves the note ringing (drum_done) or the
# track's next note takes the record over (its key_off: an FM note's release; a PCM voice only loses pcm_keyed, 0ECC-0ED9,
# and its sample plays out). An FM pitch is 40 a semitone (set_pitch 0DF0-0E0A: the high byte's low 5 bits modulo 24,
# times 4, and the top 2 bits of the low byte index note_codes, one semitone each; the low byte's other 6 bits are the
# key fraction); a PCM pitch is the K053260's (pcm_set_pitch), and a PCM drum that sets none plays its sample at the
# pitch of the sample's record (pcm_load_patch 0D3B-0D4E). PCM pans: 1 left, 4 the middle, 7 right (set_pan 0FA5, MAME's
# k053260 pan_mul).
# Which drums the songs play (scratch kits_used.py: every song's 12 tracks walked as the driver reads them, through F0,
# F1 and F6, with the kit E0 sets): of the FM kits only kit 1's drum 2 (song 80, the attract); no song sets FM kit 2. PCM
# drums no song plays: 1_D/E and 9_E/F (rests), 3_E, 5_F, 6_E, 6_F, 7_1, 7_4 to 7_D, A_3, A_4, A_E, A_F, B_E; PCM kits
# 1-11 are all set by some song.
# The PCM kits share a layout: drum 1 sample 125 or 126, drum 2 one of 127 to 130, drums 3 to 6 sample 133 or 134 at the
# pitches F40, F20, F00 and EE0 (kits 4 and 7: sample 68 on drum 3, the three lower pitches on 4 to 6), drum 7 sample 131
# or 132, drum 8 135, drum 9 139; kit 9 is another: samples 148 to 159 on drums 1 to C.

# ---- FM kit 1 (instrument_routines_low, 496C: drums 9 to F all fm_drum_1_9)
comment fm_drum_1_1 FM kit 1's drum 1: its patch at pitch D00, keyed on; fm_drum_1_1_slide then takes the pitch down for 80 passes.
comment fm_drum_1_1_slide The pitch 20 lower each pass (half a semitone), 79 times; at the 80th pass, drum_done.
comment fm_drum_1_2 FM kit 1's drum 2: its patch at pitch C70, keyed on; fm_drum_1_2_hold then counts 255 passes.
# fm_drum_1_2_hold, fm_drum_1_3_hold and fm_drum_1_4_hold only put drum_done off: the note rings on all the while, and
# only run_channel reads the record's handler (0C1B), so the count changes nothing heard
comment fm_drum_1_2_hold Counts the passes down, then drum_done; the note rings on all the while.
comment fm_drum_1_3 FM kit 1's drum 3: its patch at pitch E00, keyed on; fm_drum_1_3_hold then counts 128 passes.
comment fm_drum_1_3_hold Counts the passes down, then drum_done; the note rings on all the while.
comment fm_drum_1_4 FM kit 1's drum 4: drum 3 with another patch (the same connection, other operators) at pitch E00, keyed on; fm_drum_1_4_hold then counts 128 passes.
comment fm_drum_1_4_hold Counts the passes down, then drum_done; the note rings on all the while.
# fm_drum_1_5 to fm_drum_1_7: 768 steps of 0C take the pitch 2400 lower, past 0, and set_pitch keeps the high byte's low 5
# bits modulo 24: the slide jumps up where the high byte runs below 00 and below F8, three falls in all
comment fm_drum_1_5 FM kit 1's drum 5: its patch at pitch E00, keyed on; fm_drum_1_5_slide then takes the pitch down for 768 passes.
comment fm_drum_1_5_slide The pitch 0C lower each pass (3/16 of a semitone), for 768 passes, then drum_done; it runs off the bottom of set_pitch's range twice, and the wrap takes it back up each time.
comment fm_drum_1_6 FM kit 1's drum 6: drum 5 a major third lower (pitch D00), its patch drum 5's on both outputs; then fm_drum_1_6_slide.
comment fm_drum_1_6_slide The pitch 0C lower each pass, for 768 passes, then drum_done; it runs off the bottom of set_pitch's range twice, and the wrap takes it back up each time.
comment fm_drum_1_7 FM kit 1's drum 7: drum 5 two major thirds lower (pitch C00), its patch drum 5's on both outputs with less feedback; then fm_drum_1_7_slide.
comment fm_drum_1_7_slide The pitch 0C lower each pass, for 768 passes, then drum_done; it runs off the bottom of set_pitch's range twice, and the wrap takes it back up each time.
comment fm_drum_1_8 FM kit 1's drum 8: its patch at pitch 900, keyed on; fm_drum_1_8_slide then takes the pitch down for 768 passes.
comment fm_drum_1_8_slide The pitch 2 lower each pass (1/32 of a semitone), for 768 passes (two octaves, down to 300), then drum_done.
comment fm_drum_1_9 FM kit 1's drums 9 to F: nothing played (drum_done at once): the note is a rest.

# ---- FM kit 2 (498C: drums 6 to F all fm_drum_2_6); no song sets it. Its drums 1 to 5 never end: each goes on until
# the track's next note (or an effect) takes the record over
comment fm_drum_2_1 FM kit 2's drum 1: its patch (the right output only), then rising sweeps until the track's next note (fm_drum_2_1_setup).
comment fm_drum_2_1_setup Ten sweeps to go (count), then fm_drum_2_1_retrigger.
comment fm_drum_2_1_retrigger A sweep: the pitch from C00, keyed on, 192 passes (timer) of fm_drum_2_1_slide.
comment fm_drum_2_1_slide The pitch 2 higher each pass (six semitones in a sweep); at the sweep's end the next one (fm_drum_2_1_retrigger), and after the tenth, key off and ten more (fm_drum_2_1_setup).
# fm_drum_2_1 and fm_drum_2_2 are the same but for their patches' outputs (fm_drum_2_1_patch the right one only,
# fm_drum_2_2_patch the left one only: a stereo pair)
comment fm_drum_2_2 FM kit 2's drum 2: drum 1 on the left output (its patch is drum 1's but for that), rising sweeps until the track's next note (fm_drum_2_2_setup).
comment fm_drum_2_2_setup Ten sweeps to go (count), then fm_drum_2_2_retrigger.
comment fm_drum_2_2_retrigger A sweep: the pitch from C00, keyed on, 192 passes (timer) of fm_drum_2_2_slide.
comment fm_drum_2_2_slide The pitch 2 higher each pass (six semitones in a sweep); at the sweep's end the next one (fm_drum_2_2_retrigger), and after the tenth, key off and ten more (fm_drum_2_2_setup).
comment fm_drum_2_3 FM kit 2's drum 3: its patch, then rising sweeps until the track's next note (fm_drum_2_3_retrigger).
comment fm_drum_2_3_retrigger A sweep: the pitch from 600, keyed on, 128 passes (timer) of fm_drum_2_3_slide.
comment fm_drum_2_3_slide The pitch 2 higher each pass (four semitones in a sweep), then the next sweep (fm_drum_2_3_retrigger).
comment fm_drum_2_4 FM kit 2's drum 4: a note that wanders up and down at random until the track's next note: the random numbers from their start, its patch at pitch 1700, then fm_drum_2_4_retrigger.
comment fm_drum_2_4_retrigger Keys the note on for 6144 passes, a first random turn (fm_drum_2_4_random_turn), then fm_drum_2_4_slide.
comment fm_drum_2_4_slide The pitch_step added to the pitch each pass; below 800 a new random step up (fm_drum_2_4_random_step), from 1700 up a new one down (fm_drum_2_4_random_step_down), and a random turn when the timer runs out; after the 6144 passes, key off and fm_drum_2_4_retrigger again.
comment fm_drum_2_4_random_step A new timer and a new pitch_step up, 0 to 1F, from the next two random numbers (fm_drum_2_4_next_random).
comment fm_drum_2_4_random_step_down A new timer and pitch_step (fm_drum_2_4_random_step), turned down (fm_drum_2_4_reverse).
comment fm_drum_2_4_reverse The pitch_step negated: the wandering turns the other way.
comment fm_drum_2_4_random_turn A new timer and pitch_step (fm_drum_2_4_random_step), turned down when the next random number is odd (fm_drum_2_4_reverse).
comment fm_drum_2_4_next_random The next random number: the byte of the program's own code at random_index, which moves on by one (the same 256 bytes over and over).
comment fm_drum_2_5 FM kit 2's drum 5: its patch, then a note that climbs a semitone at a time, over and over until the track's next note (fm_drum_2_5_retrigger).
comment fm_drum_2_5_retrigger The pitch from 1500, keyed on: twelve steps (count) of 64 passes (timer) in fm_drum_2_5_slide.
comment fm_drum_2_5_slide Every 64 passes the pitch 40 higher (a semitone), 11 times; at the twelfth, fm_drum_2_5_retrigger again.
comment fm_drum_2_6 FM kit 2's drums 6 to F: nothing played (drum_done at once): the note is a rest.

# ---- the renames: fm_drum_2_4's helpers, which the guess named update, update_2... and transpose: they draw random
# steps and turn the step round (4DE5 negates +02, the step, not a pitch)
# 4DCF "call $4E04; ld (ix+$01),a; call $4E04; and $1F; ld (ix+$02),a; ld (ix+$03),$00": a timer and a step up, at random
# 4DE2 "call $4DCF", then into 4DE5 (the step negated): a random step down
# 4DE5-4DF4 "ld e,(ix+$02); ld d,(ix+$03); ld hl,$0000; xor a; sbc hl,de; ld (ix+$02),l; ld (ix+$03),h": the step negated
# 4DF8 "call $4DCF; call $4E04; bit 0,a; ret z; jp $4DE5": a random step, down when the next random byte is odd
# 4E04-4E0D "ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,$02; ld a,(hl); inc (ix+$00); ret": the next byte of the program's code at 0200-02FF

# ---- families: the chains that keep +00-+07 otherwise than the record's generic fields
# fm_drum_1_1 49CB-49D1 "ld hl,$0D00; ld (ix+$01),l; ld (ix+$02),h"; fm_drum_1_1_slide 49EA-49F9 "ld l,(ix+$01); ld
# h,(ix+$02); ld de,$0020; or a; sbc hl,de; ld (ix+$01),l; ld (ix+$02),h": the sliding pitch, at +01 (the generic one is at +02)
scoped  channel fm_drum_1_1 01 pitch u16
# fm_drum_1_5 4AD8-4ADE "ld hl,$0300; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h"; fm_drum_1_5_slide 4AFA-4B09 "ld l,(ix+$00); ld
# h,(ix+$01); dec hl; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h; ld a,l; or h; ret nz": the passes left of the slide, a word (768)
scoped  channel fm_drum_1_5 00 passes u16
# fm_drum_1_6 4B3B-4B41, fm_drum_1_6_slide 4B5D-4B6C: the same
scoped  channel fm_drum_1_6 00 passes u16
# fm_drum_1_7 4B9E-4BA4, fm_drum_1_7_slide 4BC0-4BCF: the same
scoped  channel fm_drum_1_7 00 passes u16
# fm_drum_1_8 4C01-4C07, fm_drum_1_8_slide 4C23-4C32: the same
scoped  channel fm_drum_1_8 00 passes u16
# fm_drum_2_4 4D62 "ld (ix+$00),$00"; fm_drum_2_4_next_random 4E04-4E0A "ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,$02; ld a,(hl); inc
# (ix+$00)": where in the program's bytes 0200-02FF the next random number is read
scoped  channel fm_drum_2_4 00 random_index u8
# fm_drum_2_4_random_step 4DD5-4DDD "and $1F; ld (ix+$02),a; ld (ix+$03),$00"; fm_drum_2_4_reverse 4DE5-4DF4 (negated);
# fm_drum_2_4_slide 4D8D-4D99 "ld e,(ix+$02); ld d,(ix+$03); ld l,(ix+$04); ld h,(ix+$05); add hl,de": what the pitch
# moves each pass, up or down
scoped  channel fm_drum_2_4 02 pitch_step s16
# fm_drum_2_4 4D6C-4D72 "ld hl,$1700; ld (ix+$04),l; ld (ix+$05),h"; fm_drum_2_4_slide 4D9A-4DAE "ld (ix+$04),l; ld
# (ix+$05),h; call $0DE8; ld a,(ix+$05); cp $08 ... cp $17": the wandering pitch
scoped  channel fm_drum_2_4 04 pitch u16
# fm_drum_2_4_retrigger 4D7B-4D81 "ld hl,$1800; ld (ix+$06),l; ld (ix+$07),h"; fm_drum_2_4_slide 4DB9-4DC8 "... dec hl
# ... ld a,l; or h; ret nz; call $0EBE": the passes before the note is keyed off and on again (6144)
scoped  channel fm_drum_2_4 06 passes u16

# fm_drum_2_4_random_step 4DD5 and fm_drum_2_4_random_turn 4DFB "call $4E04" (fm_drum_2_4_next_random), whose 4E09 "ld
# a,(hl)" reads the program's own byte: a random number
local   fm_drum_2_4_random_step 4DD5 a random
local   fm_drum_2_4_random_turn 4DFB a random
local   fm_drum_2_4_next_random 4E09 a random
# fm_drum_2_4_next_random 4E04-4E09 "ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,$02; ld a,(hl)": the program's 256 bytes from 0200 (sound_command's
# and start_sound's code) are the random numbers
constant fm_drum_2_4_next_random 200 RANDOM_BYTES

# ---- the FM drums' patches (load_patch 0C76-0D23: 25 bytes; the first, the connection: bit 7 the right output, bit 6
# the left, then the feedback and the algorithm. MAME wires only the YM2151's left output: a right-only patch is not heard)
# fm_drum_1_1 49C5 "ld hl,$49AC; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (connection FC: both outputs, feedback 7, algorithm 4)
49AC  table  fm_drum_1_1_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_1_2 4A18 "ld hl,$49FF; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (FC)
49FF  table  fm_drum_1_2_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_1_3 4A51 "ld hl,$4A38; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (B8: the right output only, algorithm 0)
4A38  table  fm_drum_1_3_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_1_4 4A8A "ld hl,$4A71; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (B8, other operators than drum 3's)
4A71  table  fm_drum_1_4_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_1_5 4AC3 "ld hl,$4AAA; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (BC: the right output only)
4AAA  table  fm_drum_1_5_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_1_6 4B26 "ld hl,$4B0D; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it, fm_drum_1_5_patch's but for the connection (FC)
4B0D  table  fm_drum_1_6_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_1_7 4B89 "ld hl,$4B70; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it, fm_drum_1_5_patch's but for the connection (EC:
# both outputs, feedback 5)
4B70  table  fm_drum_1_7_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_1_8 4BEC "ld hl,$4BD3; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (FC)
4BD3  table  fm_drum_1_8_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_2_1 4C52 "ld hl,$4C39; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (BC: the right output only)
4C39  table  fm_drum_2_1_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_2_2 4CB1 "ld hl,$4C98; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it, fm_drum_2_1_patch's on the left output only (7C)
4C98  table  fm_drum_2_2_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_2_3 4D10 "ld hl,$4CF7; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (F7: both outputs, feedback 6, algorithm 7)
4CF7  table  fm_drum_2_3_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_2_4 4D66 "ld hl,$4D49; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (FC)
4D49  table  fm_drum_2_4_patch  fm_patch
# fm_drum_2_5 4E27 "ld hl,$4E0E; call $0C68": the 25 bytes just before it (ED: both outputs, feedback 5, algorithm 5)
4E0E  table  fm_drum_2_5_patch  fm_patch

# ---- PCM kit 1's odd ones (instrument_routines 4E6E: drum E is pcm_drum_1_d as well)
# pcm_drum_1_b and pcm_drum_1_c play three samples one after the other, but only the first is heard: key_on (0E8B "ld
# a,($F3C7); and (ix+$4d); ret nz") does nothing to a voice pcm_keyed holds, and only key_off (0ED6) lets it go, which
# neither chain calls (music_tick's key_off comes before the chain, 4612): samples 161 and 162 are loaded (pcm_load_patch
# keys the voice off on the chip, 0D2E-0D37) and never keyed. Songs 80, 81, 8B, 99, AB and AF play them
comment pcm_drum_1_b PCM kit 1's drum B: sample 160, panned 6, keyed on; then pcm_drum_1_b_note for sample 161 and then 162, neither of them heard.
comment pcm_drum_1_b_note When the voice has ended (pcm_voice_playing): sample 161, whose key_on does nothing (pcm_keyed still holds the voice), then pcm_drum_1_b_note_2.
comment pcm_drum_1_b_note_2 When the voice has ended: sample 162, whose key_on does nothing either, then drum_done.
comment pcm_drum_1_c PCM kit 1's drum C: drum B 16 passes later (count) and panned 2, in pcm_drum_1_c_note.
comment pcm_drum_1_c_note After the 16 passes, sample 160, panned 2, keyed on; then pcm_drum_1_c_note_2 for sample 161 and then 162, neither of them heard.
comment pcm_drum_1_c_note_2 When the voice has ended (pcm_voice_playing): sample 161, whose key_on does nothing (pcm_keyed still holds the voice), then pcm_drum_1_c_note_3.
comment pcm_drum_1_c_note_3 When the voice has ended: sample 162, whose key_on does nothing either, then drum_done.
comment pcm_drum_1_d PCM kit 1's drums D and E: nothing played (drum_done at once).
comment pcm_drum_9_e PCM kit 9's drums E and F: nothing played (drum_done at once).
# pcm_drum_1_b 50B7 "ld hl,$50AE; call $0C68": the first of the three patches just before it (sample 160, level 33)
50AE  table  pcm_drum_1_b_patch  pcm_patch
# pcm_drum_1_b_note 50CF "ld hl,$50B1; call $0C68": the second (sample 161, level 33)
50B1  table  pcm_drum_1_b_patch_2  pcm_patch
# pcm_drum_1_b_note_2 50E2 "ld hl,$50B4; call $0C68": the third (sample 162, level 33)
50B4  table  pcm_drum_1_b_patch_3  pcm_patch
# pcm_drum_1_c_note 5105 "ld hl,$50EE; call $0C68": the first of the three patches before pcm_drum_1_c (sample 160, level 33)
50EE  table  pcm_drum_1_c_patch  pcm_patch
# pcm_drum_1_c_note_2 511D "ld hl,$50F1; call $0C68": the second (sample 161, level 33)
50F1  table  pcm_drum_1_c_patch_2  pcm_patch
# pcm_drum_1_c_note_3 5130 "ld hl,$50F4; call $0C68": the third (sample 162, level 33)
50F4  table  pcm_drum_1_c_patch_3  pcm_patch
# every PCM drum's patch has a decay step of 0: the voice's volume never decays (run_pcm_channel 0C07-0C12 adds 0)

# ======== the driver's variables still named after a register, by what they hold ========
# the RAM test (0930-094F "ld hl,$F000; ld bc,$0800; ld a,$55; ld (hl),a; cp (hl) ... inc hl; dec bc"): the place tested and the bytes left; the ROM test (095C-09DF "ld hl,$0000; ld bc,$0000; ld a,(bc); ld e,a; ld d,$00; add hl,de; inc bc"): the sum and the place added; self_test_failed 0B2B "ld bc,$1000; dec bc": a delay
local   sound_reset 092D,093A,0941,094E hl address
local   sound_reset 0930,093B,0944,094F bc bytes_left
local   check_rom 095C,0966,097A,098E,09A2,09B6,09CA,09DE,09E9 hl sum
local   check_rom 095F,0967,0973,097B,0987,098F,099B,09A3,09AF,09B7,09C3,09CB,09D7,09DF bc address
local   self_test_failed 0B2B,0B2E bc delay
# sound_command 0075 "ld a,c" the command; 007B/049C... "ex af,af'; ld e,a; ex af,af'; bit 0,e": the self test's failures (the other bank's A); 04CE-0523 "exx; ld a,c; exx" the sums the commands F2-F7 hand back; 0534-0826 (FE) "ld bc,$0000 ... add hl,de; dec bc" the sample ROMs' sums and the bytes left; 0231 "ld de,$0050; add ix,de" the records' stride
local   sound_command 0075 a command
local   sound_command 049C,049E,04B5,04BE,04C5,007B,04D6,04E7,04F8,0509,051A,052B,0881 a failures
local   sound_command 049D,007C de failure_bits
local   sound_command 04CE,04DF bc program_sum
local   sound_command 0554,055E,0577,0581,059A,05A4,05BD,05C7,05E0,05EA,0603,060D,0626,0630,0649,0653,066C,0676,068F,0699,06B2,06BC,06D5,06DF,06F8,0702,071B,0725,073E,0748,0761,076B,07A6,07B0,07C9,07D3,07EC,07F6,080F,0819 bc bytes_left
local   sound_command 04F0,0501 de sample_1_sum
local   sound_command 0231,0244,02EB,0303 de stride
local   sound_command 0512,0523 hl sample_2_sum
local   sound_command 053E,055D,0580,05A3,05C6,05E9,060C,062F,0652,0675,0698,06BB,06DE,0701,0724,0747,076A,0774,0790,07AF,07D2,07F5,0818,0822,0256,0315 hl sum
# start_sound 017C "ld de,$0050; add ix,de" the records' stride; 019F-01A1 "push ix; pop hl" the record of the lowest priority so far (01A6 "push hl; pop ix"); 03D9 "sub $40; ld c,a" (C0-EF) the song to play after the fade
local   start_sound 017C,018F,0290,02A3 de stride
local   start_sound 01A1,02B5 hl lowest
local   start_sound 03DB c next_song
# stop_all_channels 0103 runs into stop_music, which leaves the last record in IX (0153 "ld ix,$F373") and 0 in A (015A "xor a", what it clears the fade with), which the main loop's fade compares on (0BC9-0BCC "call z,$0106; cp $30"); sound_main 0B1F hands IX back; sound_main 0B17 "ex af,af'; ld e,a" the failures
local   stop_all_channels 0103 ix last_channel
local   stop_music 0153 ix last_channel
local   stop_music 015A a cleared
local   sound_main 0B17 a failures
local   sound_main 0B1F ix last_channel
# start_song 035D "pop af": the command again (0351 "push af"), for the song's header
local   start_song 035D a command
# sound_main_loop 0B9B "ld a,($F3CA)" (fade), 0BC0-0BC4 "ld a,($F3CB); inc a; ld ($F3CB),a" (the attenuation), 0BD1 "ld a,($F3CE)" (the next song), 0BB5 "ld hl,$F3CD; dec (hl)" (fade_timer's place)
local   sound_main_loop 0B9B a fade_kind
local   sound_main_loop 0BC3,0BC9 a attenuation
local   sound_main_loop 0BD1 a next_song
local   sound_main_loop 0BB5 hl timer_address
# pcm_load_patch 0D2E-0D34 "ld a,($F3C3); and (ix+$4c); ld ($F3C3),a" the keys; 0D50-0D5B "ld a,($F3C4); and (ix+$4e) ... or (ix+$4f)" the modes; write_operator_level 0DA7 "sla l" the carriers' mask, shifted; pcm_update_volume 0DC0-0DDB the attenuation; set_pitch 0DF0-0DF8 "ld a,h; and $1F; sub $18" the note (the high byte modulo 24); pcm_set_pitch 0E70 "ex de,hl" the pitch; pcm_key_on 0E92/0EA7 the keys; the sensitivities' bits (0F3C-0F42, 0F58-0F5A); set_pan's pans (0FAB-0FDE) and bits (0F7C-0F87); noise_on 0FF2-0FF4 "or $80; ld c,a"; next_pointer_byte 100D-1010 "ld hl,($F3C8); ld a,(hl)"
local   pcm_load_patch 0D31 a keys
local   pcm_load_patch 0D53,0D5B a modes
local   write_operator_level 0DA7 l mask
local   write_operator_level 0DB5 hl next_carriers
local   pcm_update_volume 0DC3,0DC9,0DCB,0DD1,0DD4,0DDB a attenuation
local   set_pitch 0DF1,0DF3 a note
local   pcm_set_pitch 0E70 de voice_pitch
local   pcm_key_on 0E95,0EAA a keys
local   set_channel_pm_sensitivity 0F42 c bits
local   set_channel_am_sensitivity 0F5A c bits
local   set_pan 0FB0,0FBD,0FD0,0FDD a pans
local   set_pan 0F7C,0F85 c bits
local   noise_on 0FF4 c noise
local   next_pointer_byte 1010 a value
local   next_pointer_byte 100D hl pointer
# fm_effect_13_slide 16BF-16CC "ld l,(ix+$00); ld h,(ix+$01); ld de,$01E0; add hl,de; ld (ix+$00),l; ld (ix+$01),h": the pitch's fraction and its low byte, a word of the 24-bit pitch
local   fm_effect_13_slide 16C2,16C8 hl fraction_word
# music_tick: next_track_byte's byte (42AE "call $4957"...) and the step it is kept as (42B1 "ld c,a"); 46E0 "ld b,$00" the index's high byte; 46B0/46BB "ld de,$494C"/"ld de,$4E4E" the kits' routines; 460A "ld a,(hl)" the routine's entry; the envelopes' 437F-4386 "ld d,$00; bit 7,e; dec d" the step's high byte; add_note_length 48DC-48E9 the length's code (twice the high nibble) and the length; set_note_gate 4919 "ld a,(ix+$14)" the gate, rotated out a bit at a time (4933 "rlca"), 4929 "ex de,hl" the time left, halved each turn (492F "srl d; rr e"); next_track_byte 495D "ld a,(hl)"
local   music_tick 42AE,430D,45FE,46DB,4737,473F,47B8,47BE,47C4,47CA,47EC,47F0,487E,4882,489A,489E,48B6 a byte
local   music_tick 42B1,42F3,464D,46DF,473A,4742,4763,47EF,4881,489D c step
local   music_tick 46E0 b index_high
local   music_tick 46B0,46BB de kit_routines
local   music_tick 460A hl routine_entry
local   music_pitch_envelope_1 437F,4386 d step_high
local   music_pitch_envelope_2 43BC,43C3 d step_high
local   music_pitch_envelope_3 43F9,4400 d step_high
local   music_pitch_envelope_4 4436,443D d step_high
local   add_note_length 48DD,48DF,48E0 a code
local   add_note_length 48E3,48EB de length
local   set_note_gate 4919,4933 a ratio
local   set_note_gate 4929,492F d left_high
local   set_note_gate 4929,4931 e left_low
local   next_track_byte 495D a byte
# stop_music hands back IX and A (stop_all_channels and the main loop read them): the last record and the 0
param   stop_music ix last_channel
param   stop_music a cleared
# the passes' low byte, tested with the high one (15D6 "ld a,h; or l", 1640, 1CC9)
local   fm_effect_11_pitch 15CF l passes_low
local   fm_effect_12_delay 1639 l passes_low
local   fm_effect_1f_end 1CC3 l passes_low
# start_music_channel 03D0-03D2 "ld e,(hl); inc hl; ld d,(hl)": the track's place in the song's header, a byte at a time
local   start_music_channel 03D0 e track_low
local   start_music_channel 03D2 d track_high
# sound_main runs on from the reset with the IX it was left (it hands it back when the RAM test failed: 0B1C "jp
# nz,$0B28"), else the last record stop_all_channels leaves there
local   sound_main entry ix channel

# ======== names, batch 6: what the sound program's flags say, its temporaries (build/names6/names_sound.txt; merged by the lead) ========
# batch 6, sound: names for the flags' variables and the temporaries of src/sound/ (sound_symbols.txt syntax)
# (evidence: build/names6/sound_c/listing.s and the C of src/sound/; the callers listed in work_sound.txt as "caller"
# take a source's name by themselves: none needs a line of its own)

# ======== flag sources ========

# pcm_voice_playing 1000-100C "ld a,(ix+$40); cp $08; ret c; ld a,($FC29); and (ix+$4d); ret": Z set when the
# voice's bit of the K053260's status (FC29) is clear, the sample has ended (a YM2151 record: cp $08 with A < 8 leaves
# NZ, "still playing"); the callers' "call $1000; ret nz" then stop_channel or key the sample again
# (pcm_effect_3f_00_wait_sample 2ECC: "When sample 16 has ended (pcm_voice_playing), stop_channel"; the drums' "when
# the voice has ended")
flag pcm_voice_playing z voice_ended
# the move_pitch routines end "dec (ix+$01); ret" (fm_effect_01_move_pitch 1159, 0e 1488, 1b 1AE4, 1c 1B84, 1d 1C0C,
# 26 1FDA, 29 2148): Z when the timer reaches 0 ("counts the timer down and tells whether it ran out"); the callers'
# "ret nz" go on to the next round or stop_channel only then
flag fm_effect_01_move_pitch z timer_ran_out
flag fm_effect_0e_move_pitch z timer_ran_out
flag fm_effect_1b_move_pitch z timer_ran_out
flag fm_effect_1c_move_pitch z timer_ran_out
flag fm_effect_1d_move_pitch z timer_ran_out
flag fm_effect_26_move_pitch z timer_ran_out
flag fm_effect_29_move_pitch z timer_ran_out
# fm_effect_20_move_pitch 1D3E "dec (ix+$00); ret": the same on count ("counts the count down and tells whether it ran
# out")
flag fm_effect_20_move_pitch z count_ran_out
# music_channel_song 4284 "ld a,(ix+$10); or a; ret": Z when the record plays no song; start_sound's C0-EF walk
# (zero = music_channel_song(FM_0); if (zero) ... FM_1 ...) starts the song at once only when every record says so
flag music_channel_song z no_song
# add_note_length 48EC-48F9 "ld l,(ix+$3a); ld h,(ix+$3b); add hl,de; ... ret": the length is negative (note_lengths),
# so the carry is set when time is still past 0 after the step: the next step is due at once; music_tick's rests
# (4633 "call $48DC; jp c,$42AE", back to the next byte) read on only then, as at the tempo's carry (42AD "ret nc")
flag add_note_length cy next_step_due
# pcm_effect_42_update 37B8 "dec de ... ld a,e; or d; ret" (pcm_effect_57_update 3B55, unused_effect_3f_update 28DF
# "dec hl ... ld a,l; or h"): Z when the passes are down to 0 ("true when none is left"); the callers' "ret nz"
flag pcm_effect_42_update z passes_ran_out
flag pcm_effect_57_update z passes_ran_out
flag unused_effect_3f_update z passes_ran_out

# ======== local flags ========

# write_operator_level 0DA7 "sla l; jp nc,$0DB4": the carriers' mask's top bit, set when this operator is a carrier
# (then 0DAC "add a,(ix+$45)": the volume added to its total level)
flag write_operator_level cy carrier
# fm_effect_13_slide 16BF.. and fm_effect_14_slide 17AD..: the pitch's fraction and low byte as a word plus 1E0 / C0
# ("add hl,de"), and at its carry "inc" of the pitch's high byte (AT8(...pitch, 1)++): a 24-bit sum done in parts,
# its carry into the pitch's high byte
# (left: an arithmetic carry) flag fm_effect_13_slide cy pitch_high_carry
# (left: an arithmetic carry) flag fm_effect_14_slide cy pitch_high_carry
# music_tick 42F4-42FA "ld a,(ix+$3c); or (ix+$3d); push af": Z when the previous note's gate_time is 0, which
# set_note_gate gives when gate is 0 ("the next note legato"); the AF popped after set_pitch (stack bit 6) skips the
# key off and key on and the envelope's restart: the note slurs on ("key on but for a legato note")
flag music_tick z legato
# music_tick 429B-42AD "ld l,(ix+$3a) ... add hl,de; ld (ix+$3a),l; ld (ix+$3b),h; ret nc": the tempo added to time,
# the track's next step read at its carry (field time: "the next step read at its carry"); the rests' add_note_length
# carry the same: read on while it is set (the "carry = 0u" of 42F7's "or" only goes into the pushed AF)
flag music_tick cy next_step_due
# music_note_tick 4494-44A2 "ld l,(ix+$3a) ... ld e,(ix+$3c) ... add hl,de; ret nc; jp $0EBE": time plus gate_time
# carries once the time left is no more than gate_time: then key_off ("key off once the time left is no more than
# gate_time")
flag music_note_tick cy key_off_due
# set_note_gate 492F "srl d; rr e": the time left halved, the bit that srl moves out of its high byte into the top of
# its low byte (left_low = (left_low >> 1) | (carry << 7))
# (left: the bit of a 16-bit shift) flag set_note_gate cy bit_into_low

# ======== temporaries ========

# write_operator_level 0DAC "add a,(ix+$45)": the operator's total level plus the record's volume (an attenuation),
# 7F at most (0DAF "jp p")
local write_operator_level 0DAC t3 attenuated_level
# pcm_update_volume 0DC0-0DC3 "ld a,(ix+$45); add a,(ix+$46)": the volume plus the patch's level (scoped pcm level),
# 7F at most; 0DCB "add a,(ix+$48)": that plus the decay, 7F at most
local pcm_update_volume 0DC3 t3 patch_attenuation
local pcm_update_volume 0DCB t3 decayed_attenuation
# set_pitch 0DF3-0DF5 "sub $18; jp nc,$0DF3": the pitch's high byte before 18 (8 octaves) is taken off it once more
local set_pitch 0DF3 t1 unwrapped_note
# the move_pitch routines' "dec (ix+$01)" (fm_effect_20_move_pitch "dec (ix+$00)"): the timer (count) after this pass,

# sound_main_loop 0BB5-0BB8 "ld hl,$F3CD; dec (hl); jp nz" and 0BA7-0BAA "ld hl,$F3CF; dec (hl); jp nz": fade_timer
# and fade_hold before the pass takes one off (the C tests the old value against 1)
# (left as comments: sound_main_loop is a "loop" line, not a "func" one, and check_sound_lines.py only knows func
# lines; decompile_sound.py keys local sites by place and value alone, and its names include the loop's, so the two
# lines would work once the check takes loop lines too)
local sound_main_loop 0BB8 t1 old_fade_timer
local sound_main_loop 0BAA t1 old_fade_hold
# run_pcm_channel 0C07-0C0A "ld a,(ix+$48); add a,(ix+$47)": the decay one decay_step on, 7F at most (0C0D "jp p")
local run_pcm_channel 0C0A t3 next_decay
# music_tick 42F4-42F7 "ld a,(ix+$3c); or (ix+$3d)": the previous note's gate_time, its two bytes or-ed (0 for a legato
# note)
local music_tick 42F7 t2 previous_gate_bits
# set_note_gate 4933 "rlca; jp nc,$4938": gate before this turn's rotation, whose top bit says whether the halved time
# left is added in
local set_note_gate 4933 t1 unrotated_ratio
# set_music_volume 494B-494E "ld a,($F3CB); add a,c": the music's volume plus fade_attenuation, 7F at most (494F "jp p")
local set_music_volume 494E t3 faded_volume
# check_rom 09E6-09ED "ld a,l; exx; ld c,a; exx; ld a,h; exx; ld b,a; exx" (the C's BOARD_ORIGINAL(check_rom)): the sum
# in HL while its low byte goes to program_rom_sum's (BC'), sample_rom_2_sum (HL') held while the main set is back, the
# sum again while its high byte goes over (then 09EE "ld de,$072E; sbc hl,de": the one checked), sample_rom_2_sum back
local check_rom 09E7 t3 sum_for_low_byte
local check_rom 09E9 t3 sample_2_sum_aside
local check_rom 09EB t3 final_sum
local check_rom 09ED t3 sample_2_sum_back
# sound_command F2-F7 (04CE "exx; ld a,c; exx; ld c,a; ld ($FC02),bc", 04DF b, 04F0 e, 0501 d, 0512 l, 0523 h): the
# command word in BC (its high byte the argument, echoed to FC03) set aside while a byte of the alternate set's sums
# is read, and program_rom_sum (BC') put back by the second exx
local sound_command 04CE t1 f2_command_aside
local sound_command 04D0 t1 f2_program_sum_back
local sound_command 04DF t1 f3_command_aside
local sound_command 04E1 t1 f3_program_sum_back
local sound_command 04F0 t1 f4_command_aside
local sound_command 04F2 t1 f4_program_sum_back
local sound_command 0501 t1 f5_command_aside
local sound_command 0503 t1 f5_program_sum_back
local sound_command 0512 t1 f6_command_aside
local sound_command 0514 t1 f6_program_sum_back
local sound_command 0523 t1 f7_command_aside
local sound_command 0525 t1 f7_program_sum_back
# sound_command FE 0771-0778 "ld a,l; exx; ld e,a; exx; ld a,h; exx; ld d,a; exx" (then 0779 "ld de,$93AB; sbc
# hl,de"): the sample ROM 1's sum while its low byte goes to sample_rom_1_sum (DE'), sample_rom_2_sum (HL') held while
# the main set is back, the sum again while its high byte goes over (the one checked), sample_rom_2_sum back
local sound_command 0772 t3 sample_1_sum_for_low_byte
local sound_command 0774 t3 sample_2_sum_aside
local sound_command 0776 t3 final_sample_1_sum
local sound_command 0778 t3 sample_2_sum_back
# sound_command FE 081F-0826 "ld a,l; exx; ld l,a; exx; ld a,h; exx; ld h,a; exx" (then 0827 "ld de,$A5A5; sbc
# hl,de"): the sample ROM 2's sum, its low byte for HL', the sum checked, the new sample_rom_2_sum
local sound_command 0822 t3 sample_2_sum_low_byte
local sound_command 0824 t3 final_sample_2_sum
local sound_command 0826 t3 new_sample_2_sum
# sound_command 049C-049F "ex af,af'; ld e,a; ex af,af'; bit 0,e" (F8-FD) and 007B-007E (a sound command): the
# command in A set aside while self_test_failures (A') is read, and A' put back
local sound_command 049C t1 f8_fd_command_aside
local sound_command 049E t1 f8_fd_failures_back
local sound_command 007B t1 sound_aside
local sound_command 007D t1 sound_failures_back
